&=&\frac{a_{k}}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+b_{k}\langle w^{k},x^{\star}-x^{0}\rangle+b_{k}\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&\geq&(\frac{a_{k}}{2}-\rho b_{k})\|w^{k}\|^{2}-b_{k}\|w^{k}\|\|x^{0}-x^{\star}\|\\
&\geq&\left(\frac{a_{k}}{2}-\rho b_{k}-\frac{[\eta-(1+\gamma)\beta]b_{k}^{2}}{4b_{0}}\right)\|w^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{b_{0}}{\eta-(1+\gamma)\beta}\|x^{0}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\\
&\geq&\frac{b_{0}[\eta-(1+\gamma)\beta](k+\nu-1)^{2}}{4(\nu-1)}\|w^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{b_{0}}{\eta-(1+\gamma)\beta}\|x^{0}-x^{\star}\|^{2}.\end{array}

(123)

Here, we have used
a_{k}\geq\frac{b_{0}[\eta-(1+\gamma)\beta](k+\nu-1)^{2}}{(\nu-1)}+2\rho b_{k}
and
b_{k}:=\frac{b_{0}(k+\nu-1)}{\nu-1}
in the last equality.

Finally, from (
31
) of Lemma
3
, by induction, we have

\hat{\mathcal{P}}_{k}+\tfrac{c_{k}}{2}\|Fx^{k}-u^{k}\|^{2}=\mathcal{L}_{k}\leq\mathcal{L}_{0}.

Due to the choice of
y^{-1}=x^{-1}=x^{0}
and
u^{0}:=Fx^{0}
, we get

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{L}_{0}=\frac{a_{0}}{2}\|Fx^{0}+\xi^{0}\|^{2}=\frac{b_{0}\left[\eta(\nu-1)^{2}-\beta(\nu-2)(\nu+\gamma-1)\right]}{\nu-1}\|Fx^{0}+\xi^{0}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Therefore, we eventually obtain

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{L}_{k}\leq\frac{b_{0}\left[\eta(\nu-1)^{2}-\beta(\nu-2)(\nu+\gamma-1)\right]}{\nu-1}\|Fx^{0}+\xi^{0}\|^{2}.\end{array}

We also note that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}c_{k}&=&\frac{b_{k}[(1+r)L^{2}\eta^{3}\gamma(1-\tau_{k})^{2}+r\mu\beta_{k}]}{r\mu\gamma\tau_{k}(1-\tau_{k})}=\frac{b_{0}(k+\nu-1)[(1+r)L^{2}\eta^{3}\gamma(k+\nu-1)+r\mu\beta(k+\nu)]}{r\mu\gamma(\nu-1)}\\
&\geq&\frac{b_{0}[(1+r)L^{2}\eta^{3}\gamma+r\mu\beta](k+\nu-1)^{2}}{r\mu\gamma(\nu-1)}=b_{0}C_{0}(k+\nu-1)^{2},\end{array}

where
C_{0}:=\frac{[(1+r)L^{2}\eta^{3}\gamma+r\mu\beta]}{r\mu\gamma(\nu-1)}
.

We further note that since either
r=0
or
r=1
,
\gamma=0
or
\gamma=\frac{1}{2}
, and
\mu=1
or
\mu=\frac{1}{1+\kappa}
, we can set
C_{0}:=0
if
r=0
(corresponding to Case (i)), and evaluate
C_{0}\geq\frac{2(L^{2}\eta^{3}+\beta)}{s-1}
if
r=1
(corresponding to the other cases).

Combining
\mathcal{L}_{k}\leq\frac{b_{0}\left[\eta(\nu-1)^{2}-\beta(\nu-2)(\nu+\gamma-1)\right]}{\nu-1}\|Fx^{0}+\xi^{0}\|^{2}
and (
123
), and using the above coefficients
a_{k}
,
\psi
, and
b_{0}>0
, we can show that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\|Fx^{k}+\xi^{k}\|^{2}+\psi\|Fx^{k}-u^{k}\|^{2}&\leq&\frac{1}{(k+\nu-1)^{2}}\cdot\Big[\frac{4(\nu-1)\|x^{0}-x^{\star}\|^{2}}{[\eta-(1+\gamma)\beta]^{2}}\\
&&+{~}\frac{4[\eta(\nu-1)^{2}-\beta(\nu-2)(\nu+\gamma-1)]}{[\eta-(1+\gamma)\beta]}\|Fx^{0}+\xi^{0}\|^{2}\Big].\end{array}

Here,
\psi:=0
if
\kappa=\hat{\kappa}=0
and
\psi:=\frac{4(L^{2}\eta^{3}+\beta)}{\eta-(1+\gamma)\beta}
, otherwise.
Since
\eta-(1+\gamma)\beta>0
, this inequality implies (
35
).
We note that, for Case (i), we set
\gamma=0
, and for other cases, we have used
\gamma=\frac{1}{2}
.
Hence, the final term on the right-hand side of the last inequality reduces to
\mathcal{R}_{0}^{2}
defined in (
35
) of the theorem.

\square

Appendix D
The Proof of Technical Results in Section
5

This appendix presents the full proof of the results in Section
5
.

D.1
The Proof of Lemma
5

First, since
\tau_{k}:=\frac{1}{t_{k}}
and
e^{k}:=u^{k}-Fx^{k}=z^{k}-w^{k}
, we can rewrite the second line of (
GFEG+
) as

\begin{array}[]{lcl}t_{k}x^{k+1}=\bar{x}^{k}+(t_{k}-1)x^{k}-\eta t_{k}\hat{w}^{k+1}+\beta_{k}t_{k}w^{k}+\beta_{k}t_{k}e^{k}.\end{array}

Rearranging this expression in two different ways, and using
\bar{x}^{k}=\bar{x}^{k+1}+\gamma z^{k}=\bar{x}^{k+1}+\gamma w^{k}+\gamma e^{k}
from the third line of (
GFEG+
), we get

\left\{\begin{array}[]{lcl}t_{k}(t_{k}-1)(x^{k}-x^{k+1})&=&(t_{k}-1)(x^{k}-\bar{x}^{k})+\eta t_{k}(t_{k}-1)\hat{w}^{k+1}\\
&&-{~}\beta_{k}t_{k}(t_{k}-1)w^{k}-\beta_{k}t_{k}(t_{k}-1)e^{k},\\
t_{k}(t_{k}-1)(x^{k}-x^{k+1})&=&t_{k}(x^{k+1}-\bar{x}^{k})+\eta t_{k}^{2}\hat{w}^{k+1}-\beta_{k}t_{k}^{2}w^{k}-\beta_{k}t_{k}^{2}e^{k}\\
&=&t_{k}(x^{k+1}-\bar{x}^{k+1})+\eta t_{k}^{2}\hat{w}^{k+1}-t_{k}(\beta_{k}t_{k}+\gamma)w^{k}\\
&&-{~}t_{k}(\beta_{k}t_{k}+\gamma)e^{k}.\end{array}\right.

Next, by the
\rho
-co-hypomonotonicity of
\Phi
, we have

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{T}_{[1]}&:=&t_{k}(t_{k}-1)\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{k+1}\rangle-t_{k}(t_{k}-1)\langle w^{k+1},x^{k}-x^{k+1}\rangle\\
&\geq&-\rho t_{k}(t_{k}-1)\|w^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Substituting the above two expressions into
\mathcal{T}_{[1]}
, we can show that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{T}_{[2]}&:=&(t_{k}-1)\langle w^{k},x^{k}-\bar{x}^{k}\rangle-t_{k}\langle w^{k+1},x^{k+1}-\bar{x}^{k+1}\rangle\\
&\geq&\eta t_{k}^{2}\langle\hat{w}^{k+1},w^{k+1}\rangle-\eta t_{k}(t_{k}-1)\langle\hat{w}^{k+1},w^{k}\rangle-\rho t_{k}(t_{k}-1)\|w^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}t_{k}(\beta_{k}t_{k}+\gamma)\langle w^{k+1},w^{k}\rangle+\beta_{k}t_{k}(t_{k}-1)\|w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}t_{k}\langle(\beta_{k}t_{k}+\gamma)w^{k+1}-\beta_{k}(t_{k}-1)w^{k},e^{k}\rangle.\end{array}

By Young’s inequality, for any
c_{1}>0
and
c_{2}>0
, one can prove that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{T}_{[3]}&:=&-\langle(\beta_{k}t_{k}+\gamma)w^{k+1}-\beta_{k}(t_{k}-1)w^{k},e^{k}\rangle\\
&=&-(\beta_{k}t_{k}+\gamma)\langle w^{k+1}-w^{k},e^{k}\rangle-(\gamma+\beta_{k})\langle w^{k},e^{k}\rangle\\
&\geq&-c_{1}(\beta_{k}t_{k}+\gamma)\|w^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}-c_{2}(\gamma+\beta_{k})\|w^{k}\|^{2}-\big(\frac{\beta_{k}t_{k}+\gamma}{4c_{1}}+\frac{\gamma+\beta_{k}}{4c_{2}}\big)\|e^{k}\|^{2}\\
&=&-c_{1}(\beta_{k}t_{k}+\gamma)\|w^{k+1}\|^{2}-\big[c_{1}(\beta_{k}t_{k}+\gamma)+c_{2}(\gamma+\beta_{k})\big]\|w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}2c_{1}(\beta_{k}t_{k}+\gamma)\langle w^{k+1},w^{k}\rangle-\big(\frac{\beta_{k}t_{k}+\gamma}{4c_{1}}+\frac{\gamma+\beta_{k}}{4c_{2}}\big)\|e^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Substituting
\mathcal{T}_{[3]}
into
\mathcal{T}_{[2]}
, we can derive that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{T}_{[4]}&:=&(t_{k}-1)\langle w^{k},x^{k}-\bar{x}^{k}\rangle-t_{k}\langle w^{k+1},x^{k+1}-\bar{x}^{k+1}\rangle\\
&\geq&\eta t_{k}^{2}\langle\hat{w}^{k+1},w^{k+1}\rangle-\eta t_{k}(t_{k}-1)\langle\hat{w}^{k+1},w^{k}\rangle\\
&&-{~}t_{k}\big[\rho(t_{k}-1)+c_{1}(\beta_{k}t_{k}+\gamma)+c_{2}(\gamma+\beta_{k})-\beta_{k}(t_{k}-1)\big]\|w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}t_{k}\big[\rho(t_{k}-1)+c_{1}(\beta_{k}t_{k}+\gamma)\big]\|w^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}t_{k}\big[(1-2c_{1})(\beta_{k}t_{k}+\gamma)-2\rho(t_{k}-1)\big]\langle w^{k+1},w^{k}\rangle\\
&&-{~}\frac{t_{k}}{4}\big(\frac{\beta_{k}t_{k}+\gamma}{c_{1}}+\frac{\gamma+\beta_{k}}{c_{2}}\big)\|e^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

For any
c_{1}\in(0,1/2)
, since
\beta_{k}
is chosen as in (
46
), we have
(1-2c_{1})(\beta_{k}t_{k}+\gamma)-2\rho(t_{k}-1)=0
.
Substituting this expression into
\mathcal{T}_{[4]}
and using
\beta_{k}=\frac{2\rho(t_{k}-1)}{(1-2c_{1})t_{k}}-\frac{\gamma}{t_{k}}
from (
46
), we can simplify it as

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{T}_{[4]}&\geq&\eta t_{k}^{2}\langle\hat{w}^{k+1},w^{k+1}\rangle-\eta t_{k}(t_{k}-1)\langle\hat{w}^{k+1},w^{k}\rangle\\
&&-{~}\big[\gamma(1+c_{2})(t_{k}-1)-\frac{\rho(t_{k}-1)[t_{k}-2(1+c_{2})]}{1-2c_{1}}\big]\|w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}\frac{\rho t_{k}(t_{k}-1)}{1-2c_{1}}\|w^{k+1}\|^{2}-\frac{(t_{k}-1)}{4(1-2c_{1})}\big[\frac{2\rho t_{k}}{c_{1}}+\frac{2\rho+\gamma(1-2c_{1})}{c_{2}}\big]\|e^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Since
t_{k-1}-t_{k}+1=1-\mu
due to (
46
), adding
(1-\mu)\langle w^{k},x^{k}-\bar{x}^{k}\rangle
to both sides of
\mathcal{T}_{[4]}
and using the identity

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\eta t_{k}(t_{k}-1)\langle\hat{w}^{k+1},w^{k}\rangle&=&\frac{\eta t_{k}^{2}}{2}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}+\frac{\eta(t_{k}-1)^{2}}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{\eta}{2}\|t_{k}\hat{w}^{k+1}-(t_{k}-1)w^{k}\|^{2}\end{array}

into
\mathcal{T}_{[4]}
, we can show that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{T}_{[5]}&:=&t_{k-1}\langle w^{k},x^{k}-\bar{x}^{k}\rangle-t_{k}\langle w^{k+1},x^{k+1}-\bar{x}^{k+1}\rangle\\
&\geq&\frac{\eta}{2}\|t_{k}\hat{w}^{k+1}-(t_{k}-1)w^{k}\|^{2}+(1-\mu)\langle w^{k},x^{k}-\bar{x}^{k}\rangle\\
&&-{~}\big[\frac{\eta(t_{k}-1)^{2}}{2}+\gamma(1+c_{2})(t_{k}-1)-\frac{\rho(t_{k}-1)[t_{k}-2(1+c_{2})]}{1-2c_{1}}\big]\|w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}\frac{\rho t_{k}(t_{k}-1)}{1-2c_{1}}\|w^{k+1}\|^{2}-\frac{(t_{k}-1)}{4(1-2c_{1})}\big[\frac{2\rho t_{k}}{c_{1}}+\frac{2\rho+\gamma(1-2c_{1})}{c_{2}}\big]\|e^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\eta t_{k}^{2}\langle\hat{w}^{k+1},w^{k+1}\rangle-\frac{\eta t_{k}^{2}}{2}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Now, since
\hat{\eta}_{k}=\eta(1-\tau_{k})=\frac{\eta(t_{k}-1)}{t_{k}}
due to (
46
), from the first and second lines of (
GFEG+
), we can show that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}t_{k}(x^{k+1}-y^{k})=-\eta t_{k}\hat{w}^{k+1}+t_{k}\hat{\eta}_{k}z^{k}=-\eta\left[t_{k}\hat{w}^{k+1}-(t_{k}-1)w^{k}-(t_{k}-1)e^{k}\right].\end{array}

By the
L
-Lipschitz continuity of
F
, for any
\omega\geq 0
,
c>0
, and
M^{2}:=(1+\omega)(1+c)L^{2}
, applying Young’s inequality, the last expression leads to

\begin{array}[]{lcl}(1+\omega)t_{k}^{2}\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}&=&(1+\omega)t_{k}^{2}\|Fx^{k+1}-Fy^{k}\|^{2}\\
&\leq&(1+\omega)L^{2}\|t_{k}(x^{k+1}-y^{k})\|^{2}\\
&=&(1+\omega)L^{2}\eta^{2}\|t_{k}\hat{w}^{k+1}-(t_{k}-1)w^{k}-(t_{k}-1)e^{k}\|^{2}\\
&\leq&M^{2}\eta^{2}\|t_{k}\hat{w}^{k+1}-(t_{k}-1)w^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{M^{2}\eta^{2}(t_{k}-1)^{2}}{c}\|e^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

This inequality implies that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}0&\geq&\frac{\eta t_{k}^{2}}{2}\|w^{k+1}\|^{2}+\frac{\eta t_{k}^{2}}{2}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}-\eta t_{k}^{2}\langle w^{k+1},\hat{w}^{k+1}\rangle+\frac{\eta\omega t_{k}^{2}}{2}\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}\frac{M^{2}\eta^{3}}{2}\|t_{k}\hat{w}^{k+1}-(t_{k}-1)w^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{M^{2}\eta^{3}(t_{k}-1)^{2}}{2c}\|e^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Adding this inequality to
\mathcal{T}_{[5]}
above, we get

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{T}_{[5]}&:=&t_{k-1}\langle w^{k},x^{k}-\bar{x}^{k}\rangle-t_{k}\langle w^{k+1},x^{k+1}-\bar{x}^{k+1}\rangle\\
&\geq&\frac{\omega\eta t_{k}^{2}}{2}\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}+\frac{\eta(1-M{{}^{2}}\eta^{2})}{2}\|t_{k}\hat{w}^{k+1}-(t_{k}-1)w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}(1-\mu)\langle w^{k},x^{k}-\bar{x}^{k}\rangle+\frac{t_{k}}{2}\big[\eta t_{k}-\frac{2\rho(t_{k}-1)}{1-2c_{1}}\big]\|w^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}\big[\frac{\eta(t_{k}-1)^{2}}{2}+\gamma(1+c_{2})(t_{k}-1)-\frac{\rho(t_{k}-1)[t_{k}-2(1+c_{2})]}{1-2c_{1}}\big]\|w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}\Big\{\frac{M^{2}\eta^{3}(t_{k}-1)^{2}}{2c}+\frac{(t_{k}-1)}{4(1-2c_{1})}\big[\frac{2\rho t_{k}}{c_{1}}+\frac{2\rho+\gamma(1-2c_{1})}{c_{2}}\big]\Big\}\|e^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Next, since
\bar{x}^{k+1}-\bar{x}^{k}=-\gamma z^{k}=-\gamma(w^{k}+e^{k})
from the third line of (
GFEG+
), for any
\hat{c}>0
, by Young’s inequality, we can prove that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{T}_{[6]}&:=&\frac{(1-\mu)}{2\gamma}\|\bar{x}^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}-\frac{(1-\mu)}{2\gamma}\|\bar{x}^{k+1}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\\
&=&-\frac{(1-\mu)}{\gamma}\langle\bar{x}^{k+1}-\bar{x}^{k},\bar{x}^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle-\frac{(1-\mu)}{2\gamma}\|\bar{x}^{k+1}-\bar{x}^{k}\|^{2}\\
&=&(1-\mu)\langle w^{k}+e^{k},\bar{x}^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle-\frac{(1-\mu)\gamma}{2}\|w^{k}+e^{k}\|^{2}\\
&\geq&(1-\mu)\langle w^{k},\bar{x}^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle+(1-\mu)\langle e^{k},\bar{x}^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&&-{~}\frac{(1+\hat{c})(1-\mu)\gamma}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{(1+\hat{c})(1-\mu)\gamma}{2\hat{c}}\|e^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Summing up the last two expressions
\mathcal{T}_{[5]}
and
\mathcal{T}_{[6]}
, we get

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{T}_{[7]}&:=&t_{k-1}\langle w^{k},x^{k}-\bar{x}^{k}\rangle-t_{k}\langle w^{k+1},x^{k+1}-\bar{x}^{k+1}\rangle\\
&&+{~}\frac{(1-\mu)}{2\gamma}\|\bar{x}^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}-\frac{(1-\mu)}{2\gamma}\|\bar{x}^{k+1}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\\
&\geq&\frac{\omega\eta t_{k}^{2}}{2}\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}+\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\eta}(1-M^{2}\eta^{2})}{2}\|t_{k}\hat{w}^{k+1}-(t_{k}-1)w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{t_{k}}{2}\big[\eta t_{k}-\frac{2\rho(t_{k}-1)}{1-2c_{1}}\big]\|w^{k+1}\|^{2}-\frac{\hat{a}_{k}}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}\frac{1}{2}\Big\{\frac{M^{2}\eta^{3}(t_{k}-1)^{2}}{c}+\frac{(t_{k}-1)}{2(1-2c_{1})}\big[\frac{2\rho t_{k}}{c_{1}}+\frac{2\rho+\gamma(1-2c_{1})}{c_{2}}\big]+\frac{(1+\hat{c})(1-\mu)\gamma}{\hat{c}}\Big\}\|e^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}(1-\mu)\langle e^{k},\bar{x}^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle+(1-\mu)\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle.\end{array}

where
\hat{a}_{k}:=\eta(t_{k}-1)^{2}+2\gamma(1+c_{2})(t_{k}-1)-\frac{2\rho(t_{k}-1)[t_{k}-2(1+c_{2})]}{1-2c_{1}}+(1+\hat{c})(1-\mu)\gamma
.
Rearranging this expression and using
\mathcal{V}_{k}
from (
45
), we obtain (
47
).

\square

D.2
The Proof of Lemma
6

From the
\rho
-co-hypomonotonicity of
\Phi
and
x^{\star}\in\mathrm{zer}(\Phi)
, we can show that
\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\geq-\rho\|w^{k}\|^{2}
.
Using this inequality and
\mathcal{V}_{k}
from (
45
), for any
\tilde{c}>0
, we have

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{V}_{k}&:=&\frac{a_{k}}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+t_{k-1}\langle w^{k},x^{k}-\bar{x}^{k}\rangle+\frac{(1-\mu)}{2\gamma}\|\bar{x}^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\\
&=&\frac{\tilde{c}\eta t_{k-1}^{2}}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}-t_{k-1}\langle w^{k},\bar{x}^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle+\frac{1}{2\tilde{c}\eta}\|\bar{x}^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}+t_{k-1}\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&&+\frac{(a_{k}-\tilde{c}\eta t_{k-1}^{2})}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{[(1-\mu)\tilde{c}\eta-\gamma]}{2\tilde{c}\gamma\eta}\|\bar{x}^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\\
&\geq&\frac{1}{2\tilde{c}\eta}\|\bar{x}^{k}-x^{\star}-\tilde{c}\eta t_{k-1}w^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{[(1-\mu)\tilde{c}\eta-\gamma]}{2\tilde{c}\gamma\eta}\|\bar{x}^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{(a_{k}-\tilde{c}\eta t_{k-1}^{2}-2\rho t_{k-1})}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&\geq&\frac{[(1-\mu)\tilde{c}\eta-\gamma]}{2\tilde{c}\gamma\eta}\|\bar{x}^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}+\frac{(a_{k}-\tilde{c}\eta t_{k-1}^{2}-2\rho t_{k-1})}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2},\end{array}

which proves (
49
).

\square

D.3
The Proof of Theorem
5.1

For simplicity of our analysis, let us fix
c_{1}:=\frac{1}{4}
,
c_{2}:=\frac{1}{2}
,
c:=1
, and
\hat{c}:=1
in Lemma
5
.
We also choose
\gamma:=\zeta(1-\mu)\eta
for some
\zeta\in(0,1]
.

First, from (
46
), we have

\begin{array}[]{ll}&\tau_{k}:=\frac{1}{t_{k}}=\frac{1}{\mu(k+r)},\quad\hat{\eta}_{k}:=\eta(1-\tau_{k})=\frac{\eta(t_{k}-1)}{t_{k}},\\
&\beta_{k}:=-\gamma\tau_{k}+4\rho(1-\tau_{k})=-\frac{\zeta(1-\mu)\eta}{t_{k}}+\frac{4\rho(t_{k}-1)}{t_{k}},\end{array}

as stated in (
50
).

Next, using
c_{1}=\frac{1}{4}
,
c_{2}=\frac{1}{2}
,
c=1
,
\hat{c}=1
,
\gamma=\zeta(1-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mu})\eta
, and
t_{k}=t_{k-1}+\mu
in (
47
), we can show that

\hskip-2.15277pt\left\{\begin{array}[]{lcl}a_{k}&=&\eta t_{k-1}^{2}-4\rho t_{k-1}(t_{k-1}-1),\\
\hat{a}_{k}&=&a_{k}-\psi_{k},\\
b_{k}&=&M^{2}\eta^{3}(t_{k}-1)^{2}+(t_{k}-1)\big[8\rho t_{k}+4\rho+\zeta(1-\mu)\eta\big]+2\zeta(1-\mu)^{2}\eta,\end{array}\right.\hskip-12.91663pt

(124)

where

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\psi_{k}&:=&\big[(2-3\zeta)(1-\mu)\eta-4\rho(3-2\mu)\big]t_{k-1}\\
&&-{~}(1-\mu)[(1-\zeta)(1-\mu)\eta-4\rho(3-\mu)].\end{array}

(125)

Let us choose
\zeta:=\frac{1-2\mu}{6(1-\mu)}
, provided that
0<\mu<\frac{1}{2}
.
Then, we obtain
\gamma=\frac{(1-2\mu)\eta}{6}
as stated in (
51
).
Moreover, from (
125
), to guarantee
\psi_{k}\geq 0
, we first need to choose
\eta>8\rho
as stated in (
51
).
Moreover, we can simplify
\psi_{k}
as

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\psi_{k}&=&\frac{(3-2\mu)(\eta-8\rho)}{2}t_{k-1}-\frac{(1-\mu)[(5-4\mu)\eta-24\rho(3-\mu)]}{6}\\
&\geq&\frac{(3-2\mu)(\eta-8\rho)}{2}(t_{k-1}-1+\mu)=\frac{(3-2\mu)(\eta-8\rho)}{2}(t_{k}-1).\end{array}

(126)

Hence, if
\mu r\geq 1
, then
t_{k}\geq 1
, leading to
\psi_{k}\geq 0
.

Now, utilizing (
124
), we can simplify (
47
) as

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{V}_{k}&\geq&\mathcal{V}_{k+1}+\frac{\psi_{k}}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\eta}(1-M^{2}\eta^{2})}{2}\|t_{k}\hat{w}^{k+1}-(t_{k}-1)w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{\omega\eta t_{k}^{2}}{2}\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}+(1-\mu)\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&&+{~}(1-\mu)\langle e^{k},\bar{x}^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle-\frac{b_{k}}{2}\|e^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

(127)

Since
e^{k+1}=u^{k+1}-Fx^{k+1}
, from (
41
), for any
\lambda>0
, we have

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\frac{\lambda\eta t_{k}^{2}}{2}\|e^{k+1}\|^{2}&\leq&\frac{\lambda\kappa\eta t_{k}^{2}}{2}\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}+\frac{\lambda\hat{\kappa}\eta{{}^{3}}}{2}\|t_{k}\hat{w}^{k+1}-(t_{k}-1)w^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

In addition, by the
\rho
-co-hypomonotonicity of
\Phi
and
x^{\star}\in\mathrm{zer}(\Phi)
, we have
\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\geq-\rho\|w^{k}\|^{2}
.
Combining (
127
) and the last two inequalities, and then applying Young’s inequality to
\langle e^{k},\bar{x}^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle
, for
\nu>0
, we can show that

\hskip-2.15277pt\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{V}_{k}+\frac{(b_{k}+\eta\nu t_{k}^{2})}{2}\|e^{k}\|^{2}&\geq&\mathcal{V}_{k+1}+\frac{\lambda\eta t_{k}^{2}}{2}\|e^{k+1}\|^{2}+\frac{[\psi_{k}-2(1-\mu)\rho]}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\eta}(1-M^{2}\eta^{2}-\lambda\hat{\kappa}\eta^{2})}{2}\|t_{k}\hat{w}^{k+1}-(t_{k}-1)w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{\eta(\omega-\lambda\kappa)t_{k}^{2}}{2}\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}-\frac{(1-\mu)^{2}}{2\nu\eta t_{k}^{2}}\|\bar{x}^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}.\end{array}\hskip-8.61108pt

(128)

Since
\eta>8\rho
by (
51
),
\zeta=\frac{1-2\mu}{6(1-\mu)}
, and
t_{k}=t_{k-1}+\mu
, we have from (
124
) that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}b_{k}+\eta\nu t_{k}^{2}&\leq&M^{2}\eta^{3}(t_{k}-1)^{2}+\eta\nu t_{k}^{2}+(t_{k}-1)\big[\eta t_{k}+\frac{\eta}{2}+\frac{(1-2\mu)\eta}{6}\big]+2\zeta(1-\mu)^{2}\eta\\
&=&\eta(M^{2}\eta^{2}+\nu+1)t_{k-1}^{2}+\eta S_{k},\end{array}

where

\begin{array}[]{lcl}S_{k}&:=&\big(2\nu\mu-2(1-\mu)M^{2}\eta^{2}+\frac{5\mu-1}{3}\big)t_{k-1}\\
&&+{~}\big[(1-\mu)^{2}M^{2}\eta^{2}+\nu\mu^{2}-\frac{(1-\mu)(1+4\mu)}{3}\big].\end{array}

(129)

From the last expression, if we impose

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\lambda&\geq&M^{2}\eta^{2}+2\nu+1\quad\textrm{and}\quad S_{k}\leq\nu t_{k-1}^{2},\end{array}

(130)

then we can show that
b_{k}+\eta\nu t_{k}^{2}\leq\eta\lambda t_{k-1}^{2}
.

Since
b_{k}+\eta\nu t_{k}^{2}\leq\eta\lambda t_{k-1}^{2}
, utilizing
\mathcal{L}_{k}
from (
45
), (
128
) implies that

\hskip-2.15277pt\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{L}_{k}-\mathcal{L}_{k+1}&\geq&\frac{[\psi_{k}-2(1-\mu)\rho]}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\eta}(1-M^{2}\eta^{2}-\lambda\hat{\kappa}\eta^{2})}{2}\|t_{k}\hat{w}^{k+1}-(t_{k}-1)w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{\eta(\omega-\lambda\kappa)t_{k}^{2}}{2}\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}-\frac{(1-\mu)^{2}}{2\nu\eta t_{k}^{2}}\|\bar{x}^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}.\end{array}\hskip-8.61108pt

(131)

Next, from Lemma
6
,
a_{k}
from (
124
), and
\gamma={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\zeta(1-\mu)\eta=}\frac{(1-2\mu)\eta}{6}
, we also have

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{L}_{k}&\geq&\mathcal{V}_{k}\geq\frac{[(1-\mu)\tilde{c}-\zeta(1-\mu)]}{2\tilde{c}\zeta(1-\mu)\eta}\|\bar{x}^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}+\frac{[\eta(1-\tilde{c})-4\rho]t_{k-1}^{2}+2\rho t_{k-1}}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&\geq&\frac{[6(1-\mu)\tilde{c}-(1-2\mu)]}{2\tilde{c}(1-2\mu)\eta}\|\bar{x}^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}+\frac{[\eta(1-\tilde{c})-4\rho]t_{k-1}^{2}}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

(132)

Let us first choose
\tilde{c}:=\frac{1}{2}
.
Since
\eta>8\rho
, (
132
) reduces to
\mathcal{L}_{k}\geq\frac{(2-\mu)}{(1-2\mu)\eta}\|\bar{x}^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}
.
Substituting this estimate into (
131
), we can show that

\hskip-8.61108pt\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{L}_{k+1}&\leq&\big[1+\frac{(1-\mu)^{2}(1-2\mu)}{2\nu(2-\mu)t_{k}^{2}}\big]\cdot\mathcal{L}_{k}-\frac{[\psi_{k}-2(1-\mu)\rho]}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}\frac{\eta(\omega-\lambda\kappa)t_{k}^{2}}{2}\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\eta}(1-M^{2}\eta^{2}-\lambda\hat{\kappa}\eta^{2})}{2}\|t_{k}\hat{w}^{k+1}-(t_{k}-1)w^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}\hskip-21.52771pt

(133)

Suppose that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\omega\geq\lambda\kappa\quad\textrm{and}\quad(M^{2}+\lambda\hat{\kappa})\eta^{2}\leq 1.\end{array}

(134)

Then (
133
) reduces to

\begin{array}[]{lcl}0\leq\mathcal{L}_{k+1}&\leq&\big[1+\frac{(1-2\mu)(1-\mu)^{2}}{2\nu(2-\mu)t_{k}^{2}}\big]\cdot\mathcal{L}_{k}-\frac{[\psi_{k}-2(1-\mu)\rho]}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

(135)

By Lemma
20
, for
\Lambda_{K}:=\prod_{k=0}^{K}\big(1+\frac{\theta\mu^{2}}{t_{k}^{2}}\big)=\prod_{k=0}^{K}\big(1+\frac{\theta}{(k+r)^{2}}\big)\leq\Lambda:=e^{\frac{\theta}{r}}
, where
\theta:=\frac{(1-2\mu)(1-\mu)^{2}}{2\nu(2-\mu)\mu^{2}}
, we obtain from (
135
) and Lemma
19
that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{L}_{K}&\leq&e^{\frac{\theta}{r}}\cdot\mathcal{L}_{0}\quad\textrm{and}\quad\sum_{k=0}^{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\infty}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\frac{\psi_{k}-2(1-\mu)\rho}{2}}\|w^{k}\|^{2}\leq\big(1+\frac{\theta}{r}e^{\frac{\theta}{r}}\big)\cdot\mathcal{L}_{0}.\end{array}

Note that since
u^{0}:=Fx^{0}
and
x^{0}=\bar{x}^{0}
, we have
\mathcal{L}_{0}=\mathcal{V}_{0}=\frac{a_{0}}{2}\|w^{0}\|^{2}+\frac{(1-\mu)}{2\gamma}\|x^{0}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\leq\frac{\eta\mu^{2}(r-1)^{2}}{2}\|Fx^{0}+\xi^{0}\|^{2}+\frac{3(1-\mu)}{(1-2\mu)\eta}\|x^{0}-x^{\star}\|^{2}=:\mathcal{R}_{0}^{2}
.
Combining this bound and the last expressions, we get

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{L}_{K}&\leq&e^{\frac{\theta}{r}}\cdot\mathcal{R}_{0}^{2},\\
\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}[\psi_{k}-2(1-\mu)\rho]\|w^{k}\|^{2}&\leq&2\big(1+\frac{\theta}{r}e^{\frac{\theta}{r}}\big)\cdot\mathcal{R}_{0}^{2}.\end{array}

(136)

If we choose
\tilde{c}=\frac{1-2\mu}{6(1-\mu)}
in (
132
), then
\mathcal{L}_{k}\geq\frac{[\eta(5-4\mu)-24\rho(1-\mu)]\mu^{2}(k+r-1)^{2}}{12(1-\mu)}\|w^{k}\|^{2}\geq\frac{\mu^{2}\eta(k+r-1)^{2}}{6}\|w^{k}\|^{2}
since
\eta\geq 8\rho
and
\mu\in(0,\frac{1}{2})
.
Combining this bound and the first line of (
136
), we obtain (
52
).

From (
126
), one can also show that
\psi_{k}-2(1-\mu)\rho\geq\frac{(3-2\mu){\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(\eta-8\rho)}}{2}(t_{k}-1)
.
Using this bound into the second line of (
136
), we obtain (
53
).

Finally, let us choose
\lambda:=\frac{\omega}{\kappa}
,
\nu:=1
, and
\omega:=4\nu\kappa=4\kappa
.
Then, the first condition of (
134
) automatically holds, while the second one becomes
\big[2(1+4\kappa)L^{2}+4\hat{\kappa}\big]\eta^{2}\leq 1
.
Combining this condition and
M^{2}\eta^{2}+2\nu+1=2(1+4\kappa)L^{2}\eta^{2}+3\leq\lambda=4
from (
130
), we get

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\eta\leq\bar{\eta}:=\min\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{2(1+4\kappa)L^{2}+4\hat{\kappa}}},\frac{1}{L\sqrt{2(1+4\kappa)}}\right\}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2(1+4\kappa)L^{2}+4\hat{\kappa}}}}.\end{array}

This condition is exactly the one in (
51
).
In addition, since
\nu=1
,
M^{2}\eta^{2}<1
and
\mu\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(0,\frac{1}{2})}
, the second condition of (
130
) holds if
t_{k-1}\geq 2
, which is guaranteed if we choose
r\geq 1+\frac{2}{\mu}
.
Combining this condition and
r\geq\frac{1}{\mu}
above, we get
r\geq 1+\frac{2}{\mu}
.

\square

D.4
The Proof of Theorem
5.2

First, the condition
r\geq 1+\frac{2}{\mu}
is sufficiently tight to guarantee
S_{k}\leq(1-\alpha)t_{k-1}^{2}
for some sufficiently small
\alpha\in(0,1)
instead of the second condition of (
130
).
Hence, we have
b_{k}+\nu\eta t_{k}^{2}\leq\lambda\eta t_{k-1}^{2}-\alpha\eta t_{k-1}^{2}
.
Similar to the proof of (
133
), but using this condition, we have

\hskip-8.61108pt\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{L}_{k+1}&\leq&\big(1+\frac{\theta\mu^{2}}{t_{k}^{2}}\big)\mathcal{L}_{k}-\frac{[\psi_{k}-2(1-\mu)\rho]}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{\eta(\omega-\lambda\kappa)t_{k}^{2}}{2}\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\eta}(1-M^{2}\eta^{2}-\lambda\hat{\kappa}\eta^{2})}{2}\|t_{k}\hat{w}^{k+1}-(t_{k}-1)w^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{\alpha\eta t_{k-1}^{2}}{2}\|e^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}\hskip-21.52771pt

(137)

Next, since we assume that
\eta<\bar{\eta}
in (
51
), we can show from (
134
) that
\omega>\lambda\kappa
and
(M^{2}+\lambda\hat{\kappa})\eta^{2}<1
.
Hence, we can derive from (
137
) that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\|t_{k}\hat{w}^{k+1}-(t_{k}-1)w^{k}\|^{2}&<&+\infty,\\
\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}t_{k}^{2}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}-w^{k+1}\|^{2}&<&+\infty,\\
\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}t_{k}^{2}\|e^{k}\|^{2}&<&+\infty.\end{array}

(138)

The second and third bounds of (
138
) are respectively the first two lines of (
54
).
Since
t_{k}(x^{k+1}-y^{k})=-\eta\big(t_{k}\hat{w}^{k+1}-(t_{k}-1)z^{k}\big)
due to (
GFEG+
) and
e^{k}=z^{k}-w^{k}
, by Young’s inequality, we can show that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}t_{k}\|z^{k}\|^{2}&\leq&2t_{k}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+2t_{k}\|z^{k}-w^{k}\|^{2}=2t_{k}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+2t_{k}\|e^{k}\|^{2},\\
t_{k}^{2}\|w^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}&\leq&3\|t_{k}\hat{w}^{k+1}-(t_{k}-1)w^{k}\|^{2}+3t_{k}^{2}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}-w^{k+1}\|^{2}+3\|w^{k}\|^{2},\\
t_{k}^{2}\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}&=&\eta^{2}\|t_{k}\hat{w}^{k+1}-(t_{k}-1)z^{k}\|^{2}\\
&\leq&2\eta^{2}\|t_{k}\hat{w}^{k+1}-(t_{k}-1)w^{k}\|^{2}+2\eta^{2}(t_{k}-1)^{2}\|e^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

All the terms on the right-hand side of these inequalities are summable due to (
138
) and (
53
), we get

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}t_{k}\|z^{k}\|^{2}&<&+\infty,\\
\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}t_{k}^{2}\|w^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}&<&+\infty,\\
\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}t_{k}^{2}\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}&<&+\infty.\end{array}

(139)

Now, from the first line of (
GFEG+
) and
\tau_{k}=\frac{1}{t_{k}}
, we can easily derive that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}v^{k}&:=&\bar{x}^{k}-x^{k}=t_{k}(y^{k}-x^{k})+[(\eta-4\rho)(t_{k}-1)+\gamma]z^{k}.\end{array}

Using this expression, the third line of (
GFEG+
), and
x^{k+1}-y^{k}=-\frac{\eta}{t_{k}}(t_{k}\hat{w}^{k+1}-(t_{k}-1)z^{k})
, we can derive

\begin{array}[]{lcl}v^{k+1}-\frac{(t_{k}-1)}{t_{k}}v^{k}&=&\bar{x}^{k+1}-\bar{x}^{k}-(x^{k+1}-y^{k})+\frac{[(\eta-4\rho)(t_{k}-1)+\gamma]}{t_{k}}z^{k}\\
&=&-\gamma z^{k}+\eta\hat{w}^{k+1}-\frac{\eta(t_{k}-1)}{t_{k}}z^{k}+\frac{[(\eta-4\rho)(t_{k}-1)+\gamma]}{t_{k}}z^{k}\\
&=&\eta\big(\hat{w}^{k+1}-\frac{t_{k}-1}{t_{k}}w^{k}\big)+\frac{(t_{k}-1)}{t_{k}}(\eta-4\rho-\gamma)z^{k}-\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\eta}(t_{k}-1)}{t_{k}}e^{k}.\end{array}

Let us denote
d^{k}:=\eta(t_{k}\hat{w}^{k+1}-(t_{k}-1)w^{k})+(\eta-4\rho-\gamma)(t_{k}-1)w^{k}-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\eta}(t_{k}-1)e^{k}
.
Then, the last estimate is rewritten as
v^{k+1}=\big(1-\frac{1}{t_{k}}\big)v^{k}+\frac{1}{t_{k}}d^{k}
.
By convexity of
\|\cdot\|^{2}
,
\frac{1}{t_{k}}\in(0,1]
, and Young’s inequality, we have

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\|v^{k+1}\|^{2}&\leq&\frac{t_{k}-1}{t_{k}}\|v^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{t_{k}}\|d^{k}\|^{2}\\
&\leq&\|v^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{1}{t_{k}}\|v^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{3\eta^{2}}{t_{k}}\|t_{k}w^{k+1}-(t_{k}-1)w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{3(\eta-4\rho-\gamma)^{2}(t_{k}-1)^{2}}{t_{k}}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{3{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\eta}^{2}(t_{k}-1)^{2}}{t_{k}}\|e^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Since the last three terms of this inequality are summable due to (
138
) and (
53
), one can easily prove that

\lim_{k\to\infty}\|v^{k}\|^{2}=\lim_{k\to\infty}\|x^{k}-\bar{x}^{k}\|^{2}=0,\ \ \textrm{and}\ \ \sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\tfrac{1}{t_{k}}\|v^{k}\|^{2}<+\infty.

(140)

Next, applying Young’s inequality again, we have

\begin{array}[]{lcl}t_{k}\|y^{k}-x^{k}\|^{2}&\leq&\frac{2}{t_{k}}\|v^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{2[(\eta-4\rho)(t_{k}-1)+\gamma]^{2}}{t_{k}}\|z^{k}\|^{2},\\
t_{k}\|x^{k+1}-x^{k}\|^{2}&\leq&2t_{k}\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}+2t_{k}\|y^{k}-x^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Since the two terms on the right-hand side of the first inequality are summable due to (
140
) and (
139
), we get

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}t_{k}\|y^{k}-x^{k}\|^{2}<+\infty\quad\text{and}\quad\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}t_{k}\|x^{k+1}-x^{k}\|^{2}<+\infty.\end{array}

(141)

These results lead to the third and fourth lines of (
54
).

We have proven in Theorem
5.1
that
\frac{(2-\mu)}{(1-2\mu)\eta}\|\bar{x}^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\leq\mathcal{L}_{k}\leq\mathcal{L}_{0}
.
Hence,
\{\|\bar{x}^{k}-x^{\star}\|\}
is bounded, i.e. there exists
M>0
such that
\|\bar{x}^{k}-x^{\star}\|\leq M
for all
k\geq 0
.
Using this fact and
\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\geq-\rho\|w^{k}\|^{2}
, we can deduce that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\|\bar{x}^{k+1}-x^{\star}\|^{2}&=&\|\bar{x}^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}+2\langle\bar{x}^{k+1}-\bar{x}^{k},\bar{x}^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle+\|\bar{x}^{k+1}-\bar{x}^{k}\|^{2}\\
&=&\|\bar{x}^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}-2\gamma\langle z^{k},\bar{x}^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle+\gamma^{2}\|z^{k}\|^{2}\\
&=&\|\bar{x}^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}-2\gamma\langle e^{k},\bar{x}^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle-2\gamma\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&&-{~}2\gamma\langle w^{k},\bar{x}^{k}-x^{k}\rangle+\gamma^{2}\|z^{k}\|^{2}\\
&\leq&\|\bar{x}^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}+\gamma t_{k}^{2}\|e^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\gamma}}{t_{k}^{2}}\|\bar{x}^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}+2\gamma\rho\|w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\gamma t_{k}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{\gamma}{t_{k}}\|\bar{x}^{k}-x^{k}\|^{2}+\gamma^{2}\|z^{k}\|^{2}\\
&\leq&\|\bar{x}^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}+\gamma t_{k}^{2}\|e^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{M^{2}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\gamma}}{t_{k}^{2}}+\gamma(t_{k}+2\rho)\|w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{\gamma}{t_{k}}\|v^{k}\|^{2}+\gamma^{2}\|z^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Since the last five terms on the right-hand side are summable, from this inequality, we conclude that
\lim_{k\to\infty}\|\bar{x}^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}
exists.
However, since
\lim_{k}\|x^{k}-\bar{x}^{k}\|^{2}=0
by (
140
), using the triangle inequality
|\|x^{k}-x^{\star}\|-\|\bar{x}^{k}-x^{\star}\||\leq\|x^{k}-\bar{x}^{k}\|
, we conclude that
\lim_{k\to\infty}\|x^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}
also exists.

Next, from (
135
), we can easily show that
\lim_{k\to\infty}\mathcal{L}_{k}
exists.
However, since
\mathcal{L}_{k}=\mathcal{V}_{k}+\frac{\lambda\eta t_{k}^{2}}{2}\|e^{k}\|^{2}
due to (
45
) and
\lim_{k\to\infty}t_{k}^{2}\|e^{k}\|^{2}=0
by the last line of (
138
), we conclude that
\lim_{k\to\infty}\mathcal{V}_{k}
also exists.

By (
52
), there exists
\bar{M}>0
such that
t_{k-1}\|w^{k}\|\leq\bar{M}
.
Using this inequality and
\lim_{k\to\infty}\|v^{k}\|=0
, we can show that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t_{k-1}}|\langle w^{k},x^{k}-\bar{x}^{k}\rangle|&\leq&t_{k-1}\|w^{k}\|\|v^{k}\|\leq\bar{M}\|v^{k}\|\to 0\quad\text{as}~k\to\infty.\end{array}

From (
45
), we have

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{V}_{k}=\frac{a_{k}}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+t_{k-1}\langle w^{k},x^{k}-\bar{x}^{k}\rangle+\frac{(1-\mu)}{2\gamma}\|\bar{x}^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Using the above limits, we can say that
\lim_{k\to\infty}a_{k}\|w^{k}\|^{2}
exists.
Since
a_{k}=(\eta-4\rho)t_{k-1}^{2}+4\rho t_{k-1}
, we conclude that
\lim_{k\to\infty}t_{k}^{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}
exists.
However, since
\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}t_{k}\|w^{k}\|^{2}<+\infty
due to (
53
), we can easily argue that
\lim_{k\to\infty}t_{k}^{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}=0
as stated in the first line of (
55
).
Note that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}t_{k}^{2}\|y^{k}-x^{k}\|^{2}&\leq&3\|v^{k}\|^{2}+3[(\eta-4\rho)(t_{k}-1)+\gamma]^{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}3[(\eta-4\rho)(t_{k}-1)+\gamma]^{2}\|e^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

The limit of three terms on the right-hand side are zero, leading to
\lim_{k\to\infty}t_{k}^{2}\|y^{k}-x^{k}\|^{2}=0
, which is the second line of (
55
).
The third line of (
55
) follows from Young’s inequality, the second line of (
55
), and
\lim_{k\to\infty}t_{k}^{2}\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}=0
.

Finally, since
\lim_{k\to\infty}\|x^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}
exists and
\mathrm{gra}(\Phi)
is closed (see the proof of Theorem
F.8
below), and one can easily show that any cluster point of
\{x^{k}\}
is a solution of (
NI
), we conclude that
\{x^{k}\}
converges to
x^{\star}
, a solution of (
NI
).
Since
\lim_{k\to\infty}\|x^{k}-\bar{x}^{k}\|=0
and
\lim_{k\to\infty}\|x^{k}-y^{k}\|=0
, both
\{\bar{x}^{k}\}
and
\{y^{k}\}
also converge to
x^{\star}
.

\square

D.5
The Proof of Theorem
5.3

First, since
u^{k}:=Fx^{k}
, we have
e^{k}=0
.
Thus we can choose
c_{1}=c_{2}=\hat{c}=c=0
and
\omega=0
in Lemma
5
.

In this case, from (
46
), we have
t_{k}
and
\beta_{k}
are updated as in (
56
), i.e.:

\begin{array}[]{lcl}t_{k}:=\mu(k+t_{0})=\mu(k+r),\quad\textrm{and}\quad\beta_{k}:=\frac{2\rho(t_{k}-1)-\gamma}{t_{k}}=-\gamma\tau_{k}+2\rho(1-\tau_{k}).\end{array}

In addition,
a_{k}
and
\hat{a}_{k}
in (
47
) respectively reduce to

\begin{array}[]{lcl}a_{k}&:=&t_{k-1}\big[\eta t_{k-1}-2\rho(t_{k-1}-1)\big]\quad\textrm{and}\quad\hat{a}_{k}=a_{k}-\psi_{k},\end{array}

(142)

where

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\psi_{k}&:=&2[(1-\mu)(\eta-2\rho)-\gamma](t_{k}-1)+{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(1-\mu)[(1-\mu)\eta+2\mu\rho-\gamma]}.\end{array}

(143)

Second, since
c=0
,
\omega=0
, and
e^{k}=0
, we have
M^{2}=(1+c)(1+\omega)L^{2}=L^{2}
and
\mathcal{L}_{k}=\mathcal{V}_{k}+\frac{\lambda\eta t_{k}^{2}}{2}\|e^{k}\|^{2}=\mathcal{V}_{k}
in (
45
).
Moreover, due to the
\rho
-co-hypomonotonicity of
\Phi
and
x^{\star}\in\mathrm{zer}(\Phi)
, we have
\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\geq-\rho\|w^{k}\|^{2}
.
Using these facts and (
142
), (
47
) reduces to

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{L}_{k}-\mathcal{L}_{k+1}&\geq&\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\eta}(1-L^{2}\eta^{2})}{2}\|t_{k}\hat{w}^{k+1}-(t_{k}-1)w^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{\psi_{k}}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}(1-\mu)\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&\geq&\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\eta}(1-L^{2}\eta^{2})}{2}\|t_{k}\hat{w}^{k+1}-(t_{k}-1)w^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{(\psi_{k}-2(1-\mu)\rho)}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

(144)

To guarantee the nonnegativity of the right-hand side of (
144
), we require
L\eta\leq 1
and
\psi_{k}\geq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}2(1-\mu)\rho}
.
The first condition holds if
\eta\leq\frac{1}{L}
, while the second one holds if
\eta>2\rho
,
\gamma<(1-\mu)(\eta-2\rho)
, and
t_{k}\geq 1
.
The first two conditions are stated in (
57
), while
t_{k}\geq 1
holds if
\mu r\geq 1
as stated in Theorem
5.3
.
Since
2L\rho<1
, the condition
2\rho<\eta\leq\frac{1}{L}
is well-defined.

Third, by induction, we obtain from (
144
) that

\left\{\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{L}_{k}&\leq&\mathcal{L}_{0},\quad\forall k\geq 0,\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\eta}(1-L^{2}\eta^{2})\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\|t_{k}\hat{w}^{k+1}-(t_{k}-1)w^{k}\|^{2}&\leq&2\mathcal{L}_{0},\\
\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}[\psi_{k}-2(1-\mu)\rho]\|w^{k}\|^{2}&\leq&2\mathcal{L}_{0}.\end{array}\right.

(145)

By Lemma
6
and (
45
), with
\tilde{c}:=\frac{\gamma}{\eta(1-\mu)}
, we have

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{L}_{k}&\geq&\frac{[(1-\mu)(\eta-2\rho)-\gamma]t_{k-1}^{2}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}2(1-\mu)}}\|w^{k}\|^{2},\\
\mathcal{L}_{0}&=&\frac{\mu(r-1)[\mu(r-1)(\eta-2\rho)+2\rho]}{2}\|w^{0}\|^{2}+\frac{(1-\mu)}{2\gamma}\|x^{0}-x^{\star}\|^{2}.\end{array}

(146)

Combining the first line of (
145
) and (
146
), we obtain the first line of (
58
).

Finally, since
\psi_{k}-2(1-\mu)\rho=[(1-\mu)(\eta-2\rho)-\gamma]{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(2t_{k}-1-\mu)}
due to (
143
), we obtain the second line of (
58
) from the third line of (
145
) and the second line of (
146
).

\square

Appendix E
The Proof of Technical Results in Section
6

This appendix presents the full proof of technical results in Section
6
.

E.1
The Proof of Lemma
7

First, by inserting a zero term
(t_{k}-1)\hat{x}^{k+1}-(t_{k}-1)\hat{x}^{k+1}
, we can expand
\mathcal{T}_{[1]}:=\|\hat{x}^{k}-x^{\star}+t_{k}(y^{k}-\hat{x}^{k})\|^{2}
as follows:

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{T}_{[1]}&:=&\|\hat{x}^{k}-x^{\star}+t_{k}(y^{k}-\hat{x}^{k})\|^{2}\\
&=&\|\hat{x}^{k+1}-x^{\star}+(t_{k}-1)(\hat{x}^{k+1}-\hat{x}^{k})+t_{k}(y^{k}-\hat{x}^{k+1})\|^{2}\\
&=&\|\hat{x}^{k+1}-x^{\star}\|^{2}+(t_{k}-1)^{2}\|\hat{x}^{k+1}-\hat{x}^{k}\|^{2}+t_{k}^{2}\|y^{k}-\hat{x}^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}2(t_{k}-1)\langle\hat{x}^{k+1}-\hat{x}^{k},\hat{x}^{k+1}-x^{\star}\rangle+2t_{k}\langle y^{k}-\hat{x}^{k+1},\hat{x}^{k+1}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&&+{~}2(t_{k}-1)t_{k}\langle y^{k}-\hat{x}^{k+1},\hat{x}^{k+1}-\hat{x}^{k}\rangle.\end{array}

Similarly, using
y^{k+1}-\hat{x}^{k+1}=\theta_{k}(\hat{x}^{k+1}-\hat{x}^{k})+\nu_{k}(y^{k}-\hat{x}^{k+1})
from the last line of (
GAEG
), we can expand
\mathcal{T}_{[2]}:=\|\hat{x}^{k+1}-x^{\star}+t_{k+1}(y^{k+1}-\hat{x}^{k+1})\|^{2}
as

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{T}_{[2]}&:=&\|\hat{x}^{k+1}-x^{\star}+t_{k+1}(y^{k+1}-\hat{x}^{k+1})\|^{2}\\
&=&\|\hat{x}^{k+1}-x^{\star}+t_{k+1}\theta_{k}(\hat{x}^{k+1}-\hat{x}^{k})+t_{k+1}\nu_{k}(y^{k}-\hat{x}^{k+1})\|^{2}\\
&=&\|\hat{x}^{k+1}-x^{\star}\|^{2}+t_{k+1}^{2}\theta_{k}^{2}\|\hat{x}^{k+1}-\hat{x}^{k}\|^{2}+t_{k+1}^{2}\nu_{k}^{2}\|y^{k}-\hat{x}^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}2t_{k+1}\theta_{k}\langle\hat{x}^{k+1}-\hat{x}^{k},\hat{x}^{k+1}-x^{\star}\rangle+2t_{k+1}\nu_{k}\langle y^{k}-\hat{x}^{k+1},\hat{x}^{k+1}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&&+{~}2t_{k+1}^{2}\nu_{k}\theta_{k}\langle y^{k}-\hat{x}^{k+1},\hat{x}^{k+1}-\hat{x}^{k}\rangle.\end{array}

Combining the last two expressions, we can show that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{T}_{[3]}&:=&\|\hat{x}^{k}-x^{\star}+t_{k}(y^{k}-\hat{x}^{k})\|^{2}-\|\hat{x}^{k+1}-x^{\star}+t_{k+1}(y^{k+1}-\hat{x}^{k+1})\|^{2}\\
&=&\left[(t_{k}-1)^{2}-t_{k+1}^{2}\theta_{k}^{2}\right]\|\hat{x}^{k+1}-\hat{x}^{k}\|^{2}+(t_{k}^{2}-t_{k+1}^{2}\nu_{k}^{2})\|y^{k}-\hat{x}^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}2(t_{k}-1-t_{k+1}\theta_{k})\langle\hat{x}^{k+1}-\hat{x}^{k},\hat{x}^{k+1}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&&+{~}2(t_{k}-t_{k+1}\nu_{k})\langle y^{k}-\hat{x}^{k+1},\hat{x}^{k+1}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&&+{~}2\left[t_{k}(t_{k}-1)-t_{k+1}^{2}\theta_{k}\nu_{k}\right]\langle y^{k}-\hat{x}^{k+1},\hat{x}^{k+1}-\hat{x}^{k}\rangle.\end{array}

Now, if we define the following function:

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{Q}_{k}&:=&b_{k}\langle w^{k},\hat{x}^{k}-y^{k}\rangle+\|\hat{x}^{k}-x^{\star}+t_{k}(y^{k}-\hat{x}^{k})\|^{2},\end{array}

then utilizing this function
\mathcal{Q}_{k}
and
\hat{x}^{k+1}-y^{k+1}=-\theta_{k}(\hat{x}^{k+1}-\hat{x}^{k})-\nu_{k}(y^{k}-\hat{x}^{k+1})
from (
GAEG
), we can further derive from
\mathcal{T}_{[3]}
that

\hskip-4.30554pt\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{Q}_{k}-\mathcal{Q}_{k+1}&=&\left[(t_{k}-1)^{2}-t_{k+1}^{2}\theta_{k}^{2}\right]\|\hat{x}^{k+1}-\hat{x}^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}(t_{k}^{2}-t_{k+1}^{2}\nu_{k}^{2})\|y^{k}-\hat{x}^{k+1}\|^{2}+b_{k}\langle w^{k+1}-w^{k},\hat{x}^{k+1}-\hat{x}^{k}\rangle\\
&&+{~}b_{k+1}\langle\nu_{k}w^{k+1}-\theta_{k}w^{k},y^{k}-\hat{x}^{k+1}\rangle\\
&&+{~}\left(b_{k+1}\theta_{k}-b_{k}\right)\big[\langle w^{k+1},\hat{x}^{k+1}-\hat{x}^{k}\rangle+\langle w^{k},y^{k}-\hat{x}^{k+1}\rangle\big]\\
&&+{~}2(t_{k}-1-t_{k+1}\theta_{k})\langle\hat{x}^{k+1}-\hat{x}^{k},\hat{x}^{k+1}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&&+{~}2(t_{k}-t_{k+1}\nu_{k})\langle y^{k}-\hat{x}^{k+1},\hat{x}^{k+1}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&&+{~}2\left[t_{k}(t_{k}-1)-t_{k+1}^{2}\theta_{k}\nu_{k}\right]\langle y^{k}-\hat{x}^{k+1},\hat{x}^{k+1}-\hat{x}^{k}\rangle.\end{array}\hskip-8.61108pt

(147)

Since
\theta_{k}=\frac{t_{k}-1}{t_{k+1}}
and
\nu_{k}=\frac{t_{k}}{t_{k+1}}
from (
71
), and we choose
b_{k+1}=\frac{b_{k}}{\theta_{k}}
, we have

\left\{\begin{array}[]{lclclcl}t_{k}-t_{k+1}\nu_{k}&=&0,&&\quad t_{k}(t_{k}-1)-t_{k+1}^{2}\theta_{k}\nu_{k}&=&0,\\
t_{k}-1-t_{k+1}\theta_{k}&=&0,&\text{and}&\quad b_{k+1}\theta_{k}-b_{k}&=&0.\end{array}\right.

(148)

Substituting (
148
) into (
147
), it reduces to

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{Q}_{k}-\mathcal{Q}_{k+1}&=&b_{k}\langle w^{k+1}-w^{k},\hat{x}^{k+1}-\hat{x}^{k}\rangle\\
&&+{~}b_{k+1}\langle\nu_{k}w^{k+1}-\theta_{k}w^{k},y^{k}-\hat{x}^{k+1}\rangle.\end{array}

(149)

Applying
\hat{x}^{k}=x^{k}-\lambda z^{k}
from (
GAEG
) to
{\tiny\textcircled{1}}
, the
\rho
-co-hypomonotonicity of
\Phi
to
{\tiny\textcircled{2}}
, and Young’s inequality to
{\tiny\textcircled{3}}
, for any
\hat{c}>0
, we can derive that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{T}_{[4]}&:=&\langle w^{k+1}-w^{k},\hat{x}^{k+1}-\hat{x}^{k}\rangle\\
&\overset{\tiny{\tiny\textcircled{1}}}{=}&\langle w^{k+1}-w^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle-\lambda\langle w^{k+1}-w^{k},z^{k+1}-z^{k}\rangle\\
&\overset{\tiny{\tiny\textcircled{2}}}{\geq}&-(\lambda+\rho)\|w^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}-\lambda\langle w^{k+1}-w^{k},z^{k+1}-w^{k+1}\rangle\\
&&+{~}\lambda\langle w^{k+1}-w^{k},z^{k}-w^{k}\rangle\\
&\overset{\tiny{\tiny\textcircled{3}}}{\geq}&-\big[(1+\hat{c})\lambda+\rho\big]\|w^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{\lambda}{2\hat{c}}\|z^{k+1}-w^{k+1}\|^{2}-\frac{\lambda}{2\hat{c}}\|z^{k}-w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&=&-\big[(1+\hat{c})\lambda+\rho\big]\big[\|w^{k+1}\|^{2}+\|w^{k}\|^{2}-2\langle w^{k+1},w^{k}\rangle\big]\\
&&-{~}\frac{\lambda}{2\hat{c}}\|u^{k+1}-Fx^{k+1}\|^{2}-\frac{\lambda}{2\hat{c}}\|u^{k}-Fx^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Alternatively, combining the first two lines of (
GAEG
) we get

\begin{array}[]{lcl}y^{k}-\hat{x}^{k+1}&=&\lambda z^{k+1}+\eta\hat{w}^{k+1}-\eta\gamma_{k}z^{k}\\
&=&\lambda w^{k+1}+\eta\hat{w}^{k+1}-\eta\gamma_{k}w^{k}+\lambda(z^{k+1}-w^{k+1})-\eta\gamma_{k}(z^{k}-w^{k})\\
&=&\lambda w^{k+1}+\eta\hat{w}^{k+1}-\eta\gamma_{k}w^{k}+\lambda(u^{k+1}-Fx^{k+1})-\eta\gamma_{k}(u^{k}-Fx^{k}).\end{array}

Utilizing this expression and Young’s inequality in
{\tiny\textcircled{1}}
, for any
\beta>0
, we obtain

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{T}_{[5]}&:=&\langle\nu_{k}w^{k+1}-\theta_{k}w^{k},y^{k}-\hat{x}^{k+1}\rangle\\
&=&\langle\nu_{k}w^{k+1}-\theta_{k}w^{k},\lambda w^{k+1}+\eta\hat{w}^{k+1}-\eta\gamma_{k}w^{k}\rangle\\
&&+{~}\lambda\langle\nu_{k}w^{k+1}-\theta_{k}w^{k},u^{k+1}-Fx^{k+1}\rangle-\eta\gamma_{k}\langle\nu_{k}w^{k+1}-\theta_{k}w^{k},u^{k}-Fx^{k}\rangle\\
&\overset{\tiny{\tiny\textcircled{1}}}{\geq}&\lambda\nu_{k}\|w^{k+1}\|^{2}+\eta\gamma_{k}\theta_{k}\|w^{k}\|^{2}-\left(\eta\gamma_{k}\nu_{k}+\lambda\theta_{k}\right)\langle w^{k+1},w^{k}\rangle\\
&&-{~}\eta\theta_{k}\langle w^{k},\hat{w}^{k+1}\rangle+\eta\nu_{k}\langle w^{k+1},\hat{w}^{k+1}\rangle-\frac{\beta}{\nu_{k}}\|\nu_{k}w^{k+1}-\theta_{k}w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}\frac{\lambda^{2}\nu_{k}}{2\beta}\|u^{k+1}-Fx^{k+1}\|^{2}-\frac{\eta^{2}\gamma_{k}^{2}\nu_{k}}{2\hat{\beta}}\|u^{k}-Fx^{k}\|^{2}\\
&=&\big(\lambda-\beta\big)\nu_{k}\|w^{k+1}\|^{2}+\theta_{k}\big(\eta\gamma_{k}-\frac{\beta\theta_{k}}{\nu_{k}}\big)\|w^{k}\|^{2}+\eta(\nu_{k}-\theta_{k})\langle\hat{w}^{k+1},w^{k+1}\rangle\\
&&-{~}\big[\eta\gamma_{k}\nu_{k}+\lambda\theta_{k}-2\beta\theta_{k}\big]\langle w^{k+1},w^{k}\rangle-\frac{\lambda^{2}\nu_{k}}{2\beta}\|u^{k+1}-Fx^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}\frac{\eta^{2}\gamma_{k}^{2}\nu_{k}}{2\beta}\|u^{k}-Fx^{k}\|^{2}+\eta\theta_{k}\langle\hat{w}^{k+1},w^{k+1}-w^{k}\rangle.\end{array}

Substituting
\mathcal{T}_{[4]}
and
\mathcal{T}_{[5]}
above into (
149
), and using the facts that
b_{k}=b_{k+1}\theta_{k}
and
\gamma_{k}=\frac{t_{k}-1}{t_{k}}=\frac{\theta_{k}}{\nu_{k}}
from (
71
), we can prove that

\hskip-8.61108pt\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{Q}_{k}-\mathcal{Q}_{k+1}&\geq&-{~}\frac{b_{k}}{2}\big(\frac{\lambda}{\hat{c}}+\frac{\eta^{2}\gamma_{k}}{\beta}\big)\|u^{k}-Fx^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{b_{k}}{2}\big(\frac{\lambda}{\hat{c}}+\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\beta\gamma_{k}}\big)\|u^{k+1}-Fx^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}b_{k}\big[(1+\hat{c})\lambda+\rho+\beta\gamma_{k}-\eta\gamma_{k}\big]\|w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}b_{k}\big[\frac{\lambda-\beta}{\gamma_{k}}-(1+\hat{c})\lambda-\rho\big]\|w^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}b_{k}\big[(1+2\hat{c})\lambda+2\rho+2\beta-\eta\big]\langle w^{k+1},w^{k}\rangle\\
&&+{~}\eta b_{k}\langle\hat{w}^{k+1},w^{k+1}-w^{k}\rangle+\frac{\eta b_{k}(1-\gamma_{k})}{\gamma_{k}}\langle\hat{w}^{k+1},w^{k+1}\rangle.\end{array}\hskip-30.1388pt

(150)

Next, by the Lipschitz continuity of
F
, the first line of (
GAEG
), Young’s inequality, and
z^{k}-w^{k}=u^{k}-Fx^{k}
, for any
c>0
, one can show that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}&=&\|Fx^{k+1}-Fy^{k}\|^{2}\leq L^{2}\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|^{2}\\
&=&L^{2}\eta^{2}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}-\gamma_{k}w^{k}-\gamma_{k}(z^{k}-w^{k})\|^{2}\\
&\leq&(1+c)L^{2}\eta^{2}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}-\gamma_{k}w^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{(1+c)L^{2}\eta^{2}\gamma_{k}^{2}}{c}\|u^{k}-Fx^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Denote
M_{c}:=(1+c)L^{2}
.
Partially expanding the last expression yields

\begin{array}[]{lcl}0&\geq&\|w^{k+1}\|^{2}+(1-M_{c}\eta^{2})\|\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}-M_{c}\eta^{2}\gamma_{k}^{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{M_{c}\eta^{2}\gamma_{k}^{2}}{c}\|u^{k}-Fx^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}2(1-M_{c}\eta^{2}\gamma_{k})\langle w^{k+1},\hat{w}^{k+1}\rangle-2M_{c}\eta^{2}\gamma_{k}\langle\hat{w}^{k+1},w^{k+1}-w^{k}\rangle.\end{array}

Multiplying both sides of this inequality by
\frac{b_{k}}{2M_{c}\eta\gamma_{k}}
and adding the result to (
150
), we arrive at

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{Q}_{k}-\mathcal{Q}_{k+1}&\geq&-\frac{b_{k}}{2}\big(\frac{\lambda}{\hat{c}}+\frac{\eta^{2}\gamma_{k}}{\beta}+\frac{\eta\gamma_{k}}{c}\big)\|u^{k}-Fx^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}\frac{b_{k}}{2}\big(\frac{\lambda}{\hat{c}}+\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\beta\gamma_{k}}\big)\|u^{k+1}-Fx^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}b_{k}\big[(1+\hat{c})\lambda+\rho+\beta\gamma_{k}-\frac{\eta\gamma_{k}}{2}\big]\|w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}b_{k}\big[\frac{1}{2M_{c}\eta\gamma_{k}}+\frac{\lambda-\beta}{\gamma_{k}}-(1+\hat{c})\lambda-\rho\big]\|w^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}b_{k}\big[\eta-(1+2\hat{c})\lambda-2\rho-2\beta\big]\langle w^{k+1},w^{k}\rangle\\
&&+{~}\frac{b_{k}(1-M_{c}\eta^{2})}{2M_{c}\eta\gamma_{k}}\big[\|\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}-2\langle\hat{w}^{k+1},w^{k+1}\rangle\big].\end{array}

Assume that we choose
\mu\geq 0
such that
\eta-(1+2\hat{c})\lambda-2\rho-2\beta=2\mu
.
This choice leads to
\eta:=(1+2\hat{c})\lambda+2\rho+2\beta+2\mu
as stated in (
71
).
Moreover, utilizing this relation and the following two identities

\begin{array}[]{lcl}2\langle w^{k+1},w^{k}\rangle&=&\|w^{k+1}\|^{2}+\|w^{k}\|^{2}-\|w^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2},\\
\|\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}-2\langle\hat{w}^{k+1},w^{k+1}\rangle&=&\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}-\|w^{k+1}\|^{2},\end{array}

the last inequality becomes

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{Q}_{k}-\mathcal{Q}_{k+1}&\geq&-\frac{b_{k}}{2}\big(\frac{\lambda}{\hat{c}}+\frac{\eta^{2}\gamma_{k}}{\beta}+\frac{\eta\gamma_{k}}{c}\big)\|u^{k}-Fx^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}\frac{b_{k}}{2}\big(\frac{\lambda}{\hat{c}}+\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\beta\gamma_{k}}\big)\|u^{k+1}-Fx^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}\frac{a_{k}}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{\hat{a}_{k}}{2}\|w^{k+1}\|^{2}+\mu b_{k}\|w^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{b_{k}(1-M_{c}\eta^{2})}{2M_{c}\eta\gamma_{k}}\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2},\end{array}

(151)

where

\left\{\begin{array}[]{lcl}a_{k}&:=&2b_{k}\big[(1+\hat{c})\lambda+\rho+\beta\gamma_{k}-\frac{\eta\gamma_{k}}{2}+\mu\big],\\
\hat{a}_{k}&:=&2b_{k}\big[\frac{\eta}{2\gamma_{k}}+\frac{\lambda-\beta}{\gamma_{k}}-(1+\hat{c})\lambda-\rho-\mu\big].\end{array}\right.

Finally, using (
71
), one can easily show that
a_{k}=\frac{b_{k}}{t_{k}}\big[\lambda t_{k}+(1+2\hat{c})\lambda+2\rho+2\mu\big]
and
\hat{a}_{k}=\frac{b_{k+1}}{t_{k+1}}\big[\lambda t_{k+1}+(1+2\hat{c})\lambda+2\rho+2\mu\big]=a_{k+1}
.
In this case, substituting
\mathcal{P}_{k}
from (
65
) into (
151
), we obtain (
66
).

\square

E.2
The Proof of Lemma
8

Substituting (
71
) and
b_{k}=b_{k+1}\theta_{k}
into (
66
), we get

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{P}_{k}-\mathcal{P}_{k+1}&\geq&\frac{c_{k+1}}{2}\|u^{k+1}-Fx^{k+1}\|^{2}-\frac{c_{k}}{2}\|u^{k}-Fx^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}\frac{1}{2}\big[c_{k+1}+b_{k}\big(\frac{\lambda}{\hat{c}}+\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\beta\gamma_{k}}\big)\big]\|u^{k+1}-Fx^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\mu b_{k}\|w^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{b_{k}(1-M_{c}\eta^{2})}{2M_{c}\eta\gamma_{k}}\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Since
b_{k+1}=\frac{b_{k}}{\theta_{k}}=\frac{b_{k}t_{k+1}}{t_{k}-1}
and
t_{k+1}=t_{k}+1
from (
71
), one has

\begin{array}[]{lcl}c_{k+1}+b_{k}\big(\frac{\lambda}{\hat{c}}+\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\beta\gamma_{k}}\big)=\frac{b_{k}t_{k}}{t_{k-1}}\big(\frac{2\lambda}{\hat{c}}+\frac{\eta^{2}}{\beta}+\frac{\eta}{c}+\frac{\lambda}{\beta}\big).\end{array}

Using this relation, (
60
), and
\mathcal{L}_{k}
from (
67
) into the last inequality and noting that
Fx^{k+1}-Fy^{k}=w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}
and
\frac{t_{0}-1}{t_{0}}\leq\frac{t_{k-1}}{t_{k}}\leq 1
, we can show that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{L}_{k}-\mathcal{L}_{k+1}&\geq&\frac{b_{k}t_{k}}{2t_{k-1}}\big[\frac{1-M_{c}\eta^{2}}{M_{c}\eta\ }-\kappa\big(\frac{2\lambda}{\hat{c}}+\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\beta}+\frac{\eta^{2}}{\beta}+\frac{\eta}{c}\big)\big]\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{b_{k}t_{k}}{2t_{k-1}}\big[\frac{2\mu(t_{0}-1)}{t_{0}}-\hat{\kappa}\big(\frac{2\lambda}{\hat{c}}+\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\beta}+\frac{\eta^{2}}{\beta}+\frac{\eta}{c}\big)\big]\|w^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

This exactly proves (
68
).

\square

E.3
The Proof of Lemma
9

By Young’s inequality and
z^{k}-w^{k}=u^{k}-Fx^{k}
, for any
\omega>0
, we get

\begin{array}[]{lcl}-\langle w^{k},z^{k}\rangle&=&-\langle w^{k},z^{k}-w^{k}\rangle-\|w^{k}\|^{2}\geq-\frac{2+\omega}{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2\omega}\|Fx^{k}-u^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Utilizing this inequality,
\hat{x}^{k}=x^{k}-\lambda z^{k}
from (
GAEG
), and
\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\geq-\rho\|w^{k}\|^{2}
from the
\rho
-co-hypomonotonicity of
\Phi
, we can show from (
67
) that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{L}_{k}&=&\|\hat{x}^{k}+t_{k}(y^{k}-\hat{x}^{k})-x^{\star}-\frac{b_{k}}{2t_{k}}w^{k}\|^{2}+\big(\frac{a_{k}}{2}-\frac{b_{k}^{2}}{4t_{k}^{2}}\big)\|w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{c_{k}}{2}\|Fx^{k}-u^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{b_{k}}{t_{k}}\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle-\frac{\lambda b_{k}}{t_{k}}\langle w^{k},z^{k}\rangle\\
&\geq&\frac{1}{2}\big[a_{k}-\frac{b_{k}^{2}}{2t_{k}^{2}}-\frac{[\lambda(2+\omega)+2\rho]b_{k}}{t_{k}}\big]\|w^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\big(c_{k}-\frac{\lambda b_{k}}{\omega t_{k}}\big)\|Fx^{k}-u^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

(152)

Since
t_{k+1}=t_{k}+1=k+t_{0}+1
and
b_{k+1}=\frac{b_{k}}{\theta_{k}}=\frac{b_{k}t_{k+1}}{t_{k}-1}=\frac{b_{k}t_{k+1}t_{k}}{t_{k}t_{k-1}}
,
by induction, we get
b_{k}=\frac{b_{0}t_{k}t_{k-1}}{t_{0}t_{-1}}=\frac{b_{0}t_{k}t_{k-1}}{t_{0}(t_{0}-1)}
.
Moreover, we also have
a_{k}=\frac{b_{k}}{t_{k}}\big(\lambda t_{k}+(1+2\hat{c})\lambda+2\rho+2\mu\big)
and
c_{k}:=b_{k}\big(\frac{\lambda}{\hat{c}}+\frac{\eta^{2}\gamma_{k}}{\beta}+\frac{\eta\gamma_{k}}{c}\big)=\frac{b_{k}}{t_{k}}\big[\frac{\lambda}{\hat{c}}t_{k}+(\frac{\eta}{\beta}+\frac{1}{c})\eta t_{k-1}\big]
.

Using these formulae, and choosing
\omega:=\frac{1}{4}
and
b_{0}:=\frac{3\lambda t_{0}(t_{0}-1)}{2}
, we can easily show that

\left\{\begin{array}[]{lcl}a_{k}-\frac{b_{k}^{2}}{2t_{k}^{2}}-\frac{[\lambda(2+\omega)+2\rho]b_{k}}{t_{k}}&=&\frac{b_{k}}{t_{k}}\big[\frac{\lambda t_{k}}{4}+(2\hat{c}-\frac{1}{2})\lambda+2\mu\big],\\
c_{k}-\frac{\lambda b_{k}}{\omega t_{k}}&=&\frac{b_{k}}{t_{k}}\big[\lambda(\frac{t_{k}}{\hat{c}}-4)+(\frac{\eta}{\beta}+\frac{1}{c})\eta t_{k-1}\big].\end{array}\right.

Substituting these expressions into (
152
), we obtain (
69
).

\square

E.4
The Proof of Theorem
6.1

Let us choose
c:=1
,
\hat{c}:=\frac{1}{4}
,
\beta:=\frac{\eta}{4}
, and
\mu:=\frac{\lambda+4\sigma\rho}{4}
in Lemma
8
, where
\sigma:=\frac{5}{24}
.
Then, we have
\eta:=(1+2\hat{c})\lambda+2\rho+2\beta+2\mu=2\lambda+\frac{\eta}{2}+2(1+\sigma)\rho
, leading to
\eta:=4[\lambda+(1+\sigma)\rho]
as shown in (
71
).

Using these choices, we can also show that

\Lambda:=\frac{2\lambda}{\hat{c}}+\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\beta}+\frac{\eta^{2}}{\beta}+\frac{\eta}{c}=28\lambda+20(1+\sigma)\rho+\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\lambda+(1+\sigma)\rho}
.

Since
t_{k}:=k+r
(i.e.
t_{0}=r
), to guarantee the nonnegativity of the right-hand side of (
68
), we needs to enforce the following two conditions:

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\frac{1}{M_{c}\eta}-\eta-\kappa\Lambda\geq 0\quad\textrm{and}\quad\frac{(r-1)(\lambda+4\sigma\rho)}{2r}-\hat{\kappa}\Lambda\geq 0.\end{array}

(153)

The first condition of (
153
) becomes

\begin{array}[]{lcl}4(7\kappa+1)\lambda+4(1+\sigma)(5\kappa+1)\rho+\frac{\kappa\lambda^{2}}{\lambda+(1+\sigma)\rho}\leq\frac{1}{4M_{c}[\lambda+(1+\sigma)\rho]}.\end{array}

Since
M_{c}=2L^{2}
and
\frac{\lambda}{\lambda+(1+\sigma)\rho}\leq 1
, the last condition holds if

\begin{array}[]{lcl}8L^{2}[\lambda+(1+\sigma)\rho]\big[(29\kappa+4)\lambda+4(1+\sigma)(5\kappa+1)\rho\big]\leq 1.\end{array}

This condition holds if

\begin{array}[]{lcl}4(1+\sigma)\sqrt{2(5\kappa+1)}L\rho<1\quad\text{and}\quad 0<\lambda\leq\bar{\lambda}:=\frac{2\bar{c}}{\bar{b}+\sqrt{\bar{b}^{2}+4\bar{c}}},\end{array}

where
\bar{b}:=\frac{(1+\sigma)(49\kappa+8)\rho}{29\kappa+4}
and
\bar{c}:=\frac{1-32(1+\sigma)^{2}(5\kappa+1)L^{2}\rho^{2}}{8L^{2}(29\kappa+4)}
as given in (
70
).

Since
\sigma=\frac{5}{24}
, the condition
4(1+\sigma)\sqrt{2(5\kappa+1)}L\rho<1
is equivalent to
L\rho<\frac{6}{29\sqrt{2(5\kappa+1)}}
as stated in (
71
).

Using again
\sigma=\frac{5}{24}
, we can easily bound
\Lambda=28\lambda+20(1+\sigma)\rho+\frac{\lambda^{2}}{\lambda+(1+\sigma)\rho}\leq 29\lambda+20(1+\sigma)\rho=29(\lambda+4\sigma\rho\big)
.
Thus if we choose
0\leq\hat{\kappa}\leq\frac{r-1}{58r}
as stated in (
71
), then
0\leq\hat{\kappa}\leq\frac{(r-1)(\lambda+4\sigma\rho)}{2r\Lambda}
.
Hence, the second condition of (
153
) holds.

Under the above conditions, (
68
) reduces to
\mathcal{L}_{k+1}\leq\mathcal{L}_{k}
for all
k\geq 0
.
Since
t_{0}:=r>1
,
b_{k}:=\frac{3\lambda}{2}t_{k}t_{k-1}
as computed in Lemma
9
,
c:=1
,
\hat{c}:=\frac{1}{4}
,
\mu:=\frac{\lambda+4\sigma\rho}{4}
,
\beta:=\frac{\eta}{4}
, and
\eta:=4[\lambda+(1+\sigma)\rho]
, from (
69
) we can prove that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{L}_{k}&\geq&\frac{b_{k}}{8t_{k}}\big[\lambda(t_{k}+2)+8\sigma\rho\big]\|w^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{2[6\lambda+5(1+\sigma)\rho]b_{k}t_{k-1}}{t_{k}}\|u^{k}-Fx^{k}\|^{2}\\
&\geq&\frac{3\lambda^{2}(k+r-1)(k+r+2)}{16}\|w^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

(154)

Finally, since
y^{-1}=\hat{x}^{-1}=\hat{x}^{0}:=x^{0}
, using the last line of (
GAEG
) at
k=-1
, we can easily show that
y^{0}=\hat{x}^{0}
, leading to
\mathcal{L}_{0}=\frac{a_{0}}{2}\|Fx^{0}+\xi^{0}\|^{2}+\|x^{0}-x^{\star}\|^{2}
.
Since
\mathcal{L}_{k+1}\leq\mathcal{L}_{k}
, by induction, we have
\mathcal{L}_{k}\leq\mathcal{L}_{0}=\frac{a_{0}}{2}\|Fx^{0}+\xi^{0}\|^{2}+\|x^{0}-x^{\star}\|^{2}=\frac{3\lambda(r-1)[(r+2)\lambda+2(1+\sigma)\rho]}{4}\|Fx^{0}+\xi^{0}\|^{2}+\|x^{0}-x^{\star}\|^{2}=:\frac{\mathcal{R}_{0}^{2}}{16}
.
Combining this bound and (
154
), we obtain

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\|w^{k}\|^{2}&\leq&\frac{\mathcal{R}_{0}^{2}}{3\lambda^{2}(k+r-1)(k+r+2)},\end{array}

which is exactly (
72
) after substituting
w^{k}:=Fx^{k}+\xi^{k}
.

\square

E.5
The Proof of Corollary
4

\mathrm{(i)}
Since we choose
u^{k}:=Fx^{k}
, (
60
) holds with
\kappa=\hat{\kappa}=0
.
We can choose
c:=0
,
\hat{c}:=0
,
\beta:=0
, and
\mu:=0
in Lemma
8
.
In this case, we get
\eta:=\lambda+2\rho
,
M_{c}=L^{2}
, and
c_{k}=0
.
Moreover, (
68
) reduces to

\hskip-4.30554pt\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{L}_{k}-\mathcal{L}_{k+1}&\geq&\frac{b_{k}t_{k}}{2t_{k-1}}\big(\frac{1}{L^{2}\eta}-\eta\big)\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Therefore, we can choose
0<\lambda\leq\frac{1}{L}-2\rho
, provided that
2L\rho<1
.
Furtheremore, from (
69
), we also have

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\frac{3\lambda^{2}(k+r-2)(k+r-1)}{16}\|w^{k}\|^{2}&\leq&\mathcal{L}_{k}\leq\mathcal{L}_{0}=\frac{a_{0}}{2}\|w^{0}\|^{2}+\|x^{0}-x^{\star}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Since
a_{0}:=\frac{3\lambda}{2}(r-1)[\lambda(r+1)+2\rho]
, the last inequality implies (
73
).

\mathrm{(ii)}
Since we choose
u^{k}:=Fy^{k-1}
, (
60
) holds with
\kappa=1
and
\hat{\kappa}=0
.
In this case, we choose
c:=1
,
\hat{c}:=\frac{1}{4}
,
\beta:=\frac{\eta}{4}
, and
\mu:=0
.
Then, we get
\eta=3\lambda+4\rho
and
M_{c}=2L^{2}
.
Moreover, (
68
) reduces to

\hskip-4.30554pt\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{L}_{k}-\mathcal{L}_{k+1}&\geq&\frac{b_{k}t_{k}}{2t_{k-1}}\big(\frac{1}{2L^{2}\eta}-6\eta-8\lambda-\frac{4\lambda^{2}}{\eta}\big)\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}.\end{array}

To guarantee
\Gamma:=\frac{1}{2L^{2}\eta}-6\eta-8\lambda-\frac{4\lambda^{2}}{\eta}\geq 0
, we need to impose
8\sqrt{3}L\rho<1
and
0<\lambda\leq\bar{\lambda}:=\frac{2\bar{c}}{\bar{b}+\sqrt{\bar{b}^{2}+4\bar{c}}}
as stated, where
\bar{b}:=\frac{272\rho}{123}
and
\bar{c}:=\frac{1-129L^{2}\rho^{2}}{164L^{2}}
.
Moreover, we also have
\mathcal{L}_{k+1}\leq\mathcal{L}_{k}\leq\mathcal{L}_{0}
by induction.
Using this relation and (
69
), we can conclude that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\frac{3\lambda^{2}(k+r-1)(k+r)}{16}\|w^{k}\|^{2}&\leq&\mathcal{L}_{k}\leq\mathcal{L}_{0}=\frac{a_{0}}{2}\|w^{0}\|^{2}+\|x^{0}-x^{\star}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Since
a_{0}:=\frac{3\lambda}{4}(r-1)[\lambda(2r+3)+4\rho]
, the last inequality implies (
74
).

\square

Appendix F
The Proof of Technical Results in Section
7

This appendix provides the proof of all results in Section
7
.

F.1
The Proof of Lemma
10

First, from the first line
y^{k}=x^{k+1}+\eta d^{k}
of (
GAEG+
), we have

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{T}_{[1]}&:=&\|r(x^{k}-x^{\star})+t_{k}(y^{k}-x^{k})\|^{2}\\
&=&\|r(x^{k}-x^{\star})+t_{k}(x^{k+1}-x^{k}+\eta d^{k})\|^{2}\\
&=&\|r(x^{k+1}-x^{\star})+(t_{k}-r)(x^{k+1}-x^{k})+\eta t_{k}d^{k}\|^{2}\\
&=&r^{2}\|x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\|^{2}+(t_{k}-r)^{2}\|x^{k+1}-x^{k}\|^{2}+\eta^{2}t_{k}^{2}\|d^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}2r(t_{k}-r)\langle x^{k+1}-x^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\rangle+2\eta rt_{k}\langle d^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&&+{~}2\eta t_{k}(t_{k}-r)\langle d^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle.\end{array}

Second, from the second line
y^{k+1}-x^{k+1}=\theta_{k}(x^{k+1}-x^{k})-p^{k}
of (
GAEG+
) and
t_{k+1}\theta_{k}=t_{k}-r-\mu
, we can also derive that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{T}_{[2]}&:=&\|r(x^{k+1}-x^{\star})+t_{k+1}(y^{k+1}-x^{k+1})\|^{2}\\
&=&\|r(x^{k+1}-x^{\star})+t_{k+1}\theta_{k}(x^{k+1}-x^{k})-t_{k+1}p^{k}\|^{2}\\
&=&r^{2}\|x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\|^{2}+(t_{k}-r-\mu)^{2}\|x^{k+1}-x^{k}\|^{2}+t_{k+1}^{2}\|p^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}2r(t_{k}-r-\mu)\langle x^{k+1}-x^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&&-{~}2rt_{k+1}\langle p^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\rangle-2t_{k+1}(t_{k}-r-\mu)\langle p^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle.\end{array}

Third, combining
\mathcal{T}_{[1]}
and
\mathcal{T}_{[2]}
, and the identity
\mu r\|x^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}-\mu r\|x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\|^{2}=\mu r\|x^{k+1}-x^{k}\|^{2}-2r\mu\langle x^{k+1}-x^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\rangle
, we can prove that

\hskip-8.61108pt\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{T}_{[3]}&:=&\|r(x^{k}-x^{\star})+t_{k}(y^{k}-x^{k})\|^{2}-\|r(x^{k+1}-x^{\star})+t_{k+1}(y^{k+1}-x^{k+1})\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\mu r\|x^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}-\mu r\|x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\\
&=&\mu(2t_{k}-r-\mu)\|x^{k+1}-x^{k}\|^{2}+\eta^{2}t_{k}^{2}\|d^{k}\|^{2}-t_{k+1}^{2}\|p^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}2r\langle\eta t_{k}d^{k}+t_{k+1}p^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&&+{~}2\langle\eta(t_{k}-r)t_{k}d^{k}+t_{k+1}(t_{k}-r-\mu)p^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle.\end{array}\hskip-25.83325pt

(155)

Fourth, we process the last two terms (
155
) as follows.
We first utilize
d^{k}
and
p^{k}
from (
GAEG+
) and the update rule (
77
) to show that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{T}_{[4]}&:=&\langle\eta t_{k}d^{k}+t_{k+1}p^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&=&[(\eta-\beta)t_{k}-\delta]\langle z^{k+1},x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\rangle-[(\eta-\beta)t_{k}-\eta(r-1)]\langle z^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&&-{~}[(\eta-\beta)t_{k}-\eta(r-1)]\langle z^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle.\end{array}

Next, using again (
77
), we get

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{T}_{[5]}&:=&\langle\eta t_{k}(t_{k}-r)d^{k}+t_{k+1}(t_{k}-r-\mu)p^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle\\
&=&\big[((\eta-\beta)t_{k}-\delta)(t_{k}-r-\mu)+\mu\eta t_{k}\big]\langle z^{k+1},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle\\
&&-{~}\big[\eta(t_{k}-r)(t_{k}-r+1)-\beta t_{k}(t_{k}-r-\mu)\big]\langle z^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle\\
&&+{~}\mu\eta t_{k}\langle\hat{w}^{k+1}-z^{k+1},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle.\end{array}

Then, substituting
\mathcal{T}_{[4]}
and
\mathcal{T}_{[5]}
into
\mathcal{T}_{[3]}
of (
155
), we arrive at

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{T}_{[3]}&=&\mu(2t_{k}-r-\mu)\|x^{k+1}-x^{k}\|^{2}+\eta^{2}t_{k}^{2}\|d^{k}\|^{2}-t_{k+1}^{2}\|p^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}2r[(\eta-\beta)t_{k}-\delta]\langle z^{k+1},x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&&-{~}2r[(\eta-\beta)t_{k}-\eta(r-1)]\langle z^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&&+{~}2\big[((\eta-\beta)t_{k}-\delta)(t_{k}-r-\mu)+\mu\eta t_{k}\big]\langle z^{k+1}-z^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle\\
&&-{~}2\big[((\eta-\beta)r+\delta)t_{k}-\eta(r-1)t_{k}-\delta(r+\mu)\big]\langle z^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle\\
&&+{~}2\mu\eta t_{k}\langle\hat{w}^{k+1}-z^{k+1},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle.\end{array}\hskip-12.91663pt

(156)

Fifth, exploiting again
y^{k}=x^{k+1}+\eta d^{k}
and
y^{k+1}-x^{k+1}=\theta_{k}(x^{k+1}-x^{k})-p^{k}
from (
GAEG+
), one can express

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{T}_{[6]}&=&2c_{k}\langle z^{k},y^{k}-x^{k}\rangle-2c_{k+1}\langle z^{k+1},y^{k+1}-x^{k+1}\rangle\\
&=&2c_{k}\langle z^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}+\eta d^{k}\rangle-2c_{k+1}\langle z^{k+1},\theta_{k}(x^{k+1}-x^{k})-p^{k}\rangle\\
&=&2c_{k}\langle z^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle-2c_{k+1}\theta_{k}\langle z^{k+1},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle\\
&&+{~}2\eta c_{k}\langle z^{k},d^{k}\rangle+2c_{k+1}\langle z^{k+1},p^{k}\rangle.\end{array}

Sixth, adding
\mathcal{T}_{[6]}
to
\mathcal{T}_{[3]}
in (
156
), and noticing that
\mathcal{P}_{k}-\mathcal{P}_{k+1}=\mathcal{T}_{[3]}+\mathcal{T}_{[6]}
, we further obtain

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{P}_{k}-\mathcal{P}_{k+1}&=&\eta^{2}t_{k}^{2}\|d^{k}\|^{2}-t_{k+1}^{2}\|p^{k}\|^{2}+2\eta c_{k}\langle z^{k},d^{k}\rangle+2c_{k+1}\langle z^{k+1},p^{k}\rangle\\
&&+{~}\mu(2t_{k}-r-\mu)\|x^{k+1}-x^{k}\|^{2}+2\mu\eta t_{k}\langle\hat{w}^{k+1}-z^{k+1},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle\\
&&+{~}2r[(\eta-\beta)t_{k}-\delta]\langle z^{k+1},x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&&-{~}2r[(\eta-\beta)t_{k}-\eta(r-1)]\langle z^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&&+{~}2\big[\omega(t_{k}-\mu-r)+\mu\eta\big]t_{k}\langle z^{k+1}-z^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle.\end{array}

Finally, substituting
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{E}_{k}}
from (
83
) into the last expression, we get (
84
).

\square

F.2
The Proof of Lemma
11

Exploiting
d^{k}:=\hat{w}^{k+1}-\gamma_{k}z^{k}
and
p^{k}:=\eta_{k}z^{k+1}-\lambda_{k}\hat{w}^{k+1}+\nu_{k}z^{k}
from (
76
), we can expand
\mathcal{E}_{k}
in (
83
), grouping each term and using
\lambda_{k}=\frac{\eta t_{k}}{t_{k+1}}
yield

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{E}_{k}&\overset{\tiny\eqref{eq:NGEAG4NI_Pk_and_Ek}}{=}&\eta^{2}t_{k}^{2}\|d^{k}\|^{2}-t_{k+1}^{2}\|p^{k}\|^{2}+2\eta c_{k}\langle z^{k},d^{k}\rangle+2c_{k+1}\langle z^{k+1},p^{k}\rangle\\
&=&\eta_{k}\big(2c_{k+1}-t_{k+1}^{2}\eta_{k}\big)\|z^{k+1}\|^{2}+\big(\eta^{2}t_{k}^{2}\gamma_{k}^{2}-t_{k+1}^{2}\nu_{k}^{2}-2\eta c_{k}\gamma_{k}\big)\|z^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}2\lambda_{k}\big(t_{k+1}^{2}\eta_{k}-c_{k+1}\big)\langle z^{k+1},\hat{w}^{k+1}\rangle\\
&&-{~}2\nu_{k}\big(t_{k+1}^{2}\eta_{k}-c_{k+1}\big)\langle z^{k+1},z^{k}\rangle\\
&&-{~}2\big(\eta^{2}t_{k}^{2}\gamma_{k}-t_{k+1}^{2}\lambda_{k}\nu_{k}-\eta c_{k}\big)\langle\hat{w}^{k+1},z^{k}\rangle.\end{array}

By (
77
) and (
82
), we can compute each coefficient of the last expression to get

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{E}_{k}&=&\eta^{2}t_{k}^{2}\|d^{k}\|^{2}-t_{k+1}^{2}\|p^{k}\|^{2}+2\eta c_{k}\langle z^{k},d^{k}\rangle+2c_{k+1}\langle z^{k+1},p^{k}\rangle\\
&=&\big[(1-\mu)\psi t_{k}-\delta\big][(\eta-\beta)t_{k}-\delta\big]\|z^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\big\{\eta(t_{k}-r+1)\big[(\eta-2\psi)(t_{k}-r+1)+2(r-1)\beta\big]-\beta^{2}t_{k}^{2}\big\}\|z^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}2\eta\omega t_{k}^{2}\langle z^{k+1},\hat{w}^{k+1}\rangle-2\beta\omega t_{k}^{2}\langle z^{k+1},z^{k}\rangle\\
&&-{~}2\eta\omega t_{k}(t_{k}-r+1)\langle\hat{w}^{k+1},z^{k}\rangle.\end{array}

This is exactly (
85
).

\square

F.3
The Proof of Lemma
12

First, by (
75
), (
GAEG+
), and the
L
-Lipschitz continuity of
F
, we have
\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|=\|Fx^{k+1}-Fy^{k}\|\leq L\|x^{k+1}-y^{k}\|=L\eta\|d^{k}\|
.
Using this relation, Young’s inequality, for any
c_{1}>0
,
\phi>0
, and
\hat{\phi}\geq 0
, we get

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\bar{\mathcal{T}}_{[1]}&:=&\|z^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}+\phi\|z^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}+\hat{\phi}\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&\leq&\big[(1+\phi)(1+c_{1})+\hat{\phi}\big]\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}+\frac{(1+\phi)(1+c_{1})}{c_{1}}\|z^{k+1}-w^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&\leq&\big[(1+\phi)(1+c_{1})+\hat{\phi}\big]L^{2}\eta^{2}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}-\gamma_{k}z^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{(1+\phi)(1+c_{1})}{c_{1}}\|z^{k+1}-w^{k+1}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Let
M^{2}:=\big[(1+\phi)(1+c_{1})+\hat{\phi}\big]L^{2}
as stated.
Expanding the first term on the left-hand side, and the first term on the right-hand side of
\bar{\mathcal{T}}_{[1]}
above, and then using
\gamma_{k}:=\frac{t_{k}-r+1}{t_{k}}
from (
77
), rearranging the result, we get

\begin{array}[]{lcl}0&\geq&t_{k}^{2}\|z^{k+1}\|^{2}+(1-M^{2}\eta^{2})t_{k}^{2}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}-M^{2}\eta^{2}(t_{k}-r+1)^{2}\|z^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}2t_{k}^{2}\langle z^{k+1},\hat{w}^{k+1}\rangle+2M^{2}\eta^{2}t_{k}(t_{k}-r+1)\langle\hat{w}^{k+1},z^{k}\rangle+\hat{\phi}t_{k}^{2}\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\phi t_{k}^{2}\|z^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}-\frac{(1+\phi)(1+c_{1})t_{k}^{2}}{c_{1}}\|z^{k+1}-w^{k+1}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Multiplying this inequality by
\eta\omega
with
\omega:=\frac{\mu(\eta-\beta)}{\mu+1}
, and adding the result to
\mathcal{E}_{k}
in (
85
) of Lemma
11
, we can show that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{E}_{k}&\geq&\big\{\big[(1-\mu)\psi t_{k}-\delta\big][(\eta-\beta)t_{k}-\delta]+\eta\omega t_{k}^{2}\big\}\|z^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\big\{\eta(t_{k}\!-\!r\!+\!1)\big[\big(\eta-2\psi-\omega M^{2}\eta^{2}\big)(t_{k}\!-\!r\!+\!1)+2(r-1)\beta\big]-\beta^{2}t_{k}^{2}\big\}\|z^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}2\beta\omega t_{k}^{2}\langle z^{k+1},z^{k}\rangle+\eta\omega(1-M^{2}\eta^{2})t_{k}^{2}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}2\eta\omega(1-M^{2}\eta^{2})t_{k}(t_{k}-r+1)\langle\hat{w}^{k+1},z^{k}\rangle+\eta\omega\hat{\phi}t_{k}^{2}\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\eta\omega\phi t_{k}^{2}\|z^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}-\frac{\eta\omega(1+\phi)(1+c_{1})t_{k}^{2}}{c_{1}}\|z^{k+1}-w^{k+1}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Since
t_{k}\hat{w}^{k+1}-(t_{k}-r+1)z^{k}=t_{k}d^{k}
due to (
76
), we can easily show that

\begin{array}[]{ll}&2\langle z^{k+1},z^{k}\rangle=\|z^{k}\|^{2}+\|z^{k+1}\|^{2}-\|z^{k+1}-z^{k}\|^{2},\\
&t_{k}^{2}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}-2t_{k}(t_{k}-r+1)\langle\hat{w}^{k+1},z^{k}\rangle+(t_{k}-r+1)^{2}\|z^{k}\|^{2}=t_{k}^{2}\|d^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Utilizing these two identities into the last inequality, and rearranging the result, we can further lower bound

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{E}_{k}&\geq&\hat{\Lambda}_{k+1}\|z^{k+1}\|^{2}-\Lambda_{k}\|z^{k}\|^{2}+\beta\omega t_{k}^{2}\|z^{k+1}-z^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\eta\omega(1-M^{2}\eta^{2})t_{k}^{2}\|d^{k}\|^{2}+\eta\omega\hat{\phi}t_{k}^{2}\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\eta\omega\phi t_{k}^{2}\|z^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}-\frac{\eta\omega(1+\phi)(1+c_{1})t_{k}^{2}}{c_{1}}\|z^{k+1}-w^{k+1}\|^{2},\end{array}

(157)

where
\hat{\Lambda}_{k+1}
and
\Lambda_{k}
are respectively given by

\left\{\begin{array}[]{lcl}\hat{\Lambda}_{k+1}&:=&\big[(1-\mu)\psi t_{k}-\delta\big][(\eta-\beta)t_{k}-\delta]+\omega(\eta-\beta)t_{k}^{2},\\
\Lambda_{k}&:=&2\eta(t_{k}-r+1)\big[\psi(t_{k}-r+1)-(r-1)\beta\big]-\beta^{2}t_{k}^{2}.\end{array}\right.

If we simplify
\Lambda_{k}
, then we obtain it as in (
87
).
Furthermore, we can easily prove that
\hat{\Lambda}_{k+1}=\Lambda_{k+1}+S_{k}
for
S_{k}
defined by (
87
).

Finally, using
\hat{\Lambda}_{k+1}
,
\Lambda_{k}
, and
S_{k}
from (
87
), we obtain (
86
) from (
157
).

\square

F.4
The Proof of Lemma
13

First, since
u^{k}:=Fx^{k}
in (
GAEG+
),
\mathcal{P}_{k}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{E}_{k}}
in (
83
) reduce to

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{P}_{k}&:=&\|r(x^{k}-x^{\star})+t_{k}(y^{k}-x^{k})\|^{2}+r\mu\|x^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}+2c_{k}\langle w^{k},y^{k}-x^{k}\rangle,\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{E}_{k}}&:=&\eta^{2}t_{k}^{2}\|d^{k}\|^{2}-t_{k+1}^{2}\|p^{k}\|^{2}+2\eta c_{k}\langle w^{k},d^{k}\rangle+2c_{k+1}\langle w^{k+1},p^{k}\rangle.\end{array}

In this case, (
84
) becomes

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{P}_{k}-\mathcal{P}_{k+1}&=&\mathcal{E}_{k}+\mu(2t_{k}-r-\mu)\|x^{k+1}-x^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}2r[(\eta-\beta)t_{k}-\delta]\langle w^{k+1},x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&&-{~}2r[(\eta-\beta)t_{k}-\eta(r-1)]\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&&+{~}2t_{k}\big[\omega(t_{k}-\mu-r)+\mu\eta\big]\langle w^{k+1}-w^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle\\
&&+{~}2\mu\eta t_{k}\langle\hat{w}^{k+1}-w^{k+1},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle.\end{array}

(158)

Next, since
z^{k}=w^{k}
, we can choose
c_{1}:=0
and
\phi:=0
and (
86
) reduces to

\begin{array}[]{lcl}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{E}_{k}}&\geq&\Lambda_{k+1}\|w^{k+1}\|^{2}-\Lambda_{k}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+S_{k}\|w^{k+1}\|^{2}+\beta\omega t_{k}^{2}\|w^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\eta\omega(1-M^{2}\eta^{2})t_{k}^{2}\|d^{k}\|^{2}+\eta\omega\hat{\phi}t_{k}^{2}\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2},\end{array}

(159)

where
M^{2}:=\big(1+\hat{\phi})L^{2}
for any
\hat{\phi}\geq 0
.

Now, substituting (
159
) into (
158
), and using
\langle w^{k+1}-w^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle\geq-\rho\|w^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}
from the
\rho
-co-hypomonotonicity of
\Phi
, we get

\hskip-4.30554pt\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{P}_{k}-\mathcal{P}_{k+1}&\geq&\Lambda_{k+1}\|w^{k+1}\|^{2}-\Lambda_{k}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+\mu(2t_{k}-r-\mu)\|x^{k+1}-x^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}2r[(\eta-\beta)t_{k+1}-\eta(r-1)]\langle w^{k+1},x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&&-{~}2r[(\eta-\beta)t_{k}-\eta(r-1)]\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&&+{~}2r(r-2)\omega\langle w^{k+1},x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\rangle+S_{k}\|w^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}t_{k}\big\{\beta\omega t_{k}-2\rho\big[\omega(t_{k}-\mu-r)+\mu\eta\big]\big\}\|w^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\eta\omega(1-M^{2}\eta^{2})t_{k}^{2}\|d^{k}\|^{2}+\eta\omega\hat{\phi}t_{k}^{2}\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}2\mu\eta t_{k}\langle\hat{w}^{k+1}-w^{k+1},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle.\end{array}\hskip-17.22217pt

(160)

Utilizing
\mathcal{L}_{k}
from (
88
), and Young’s inequality for the last term of (
160
), we can show from this expression that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{L}_{k}-\mathcal{L}_{k+1}&\geq&\mu\big(2t_{k}-r-\mu-\frac{\mu\eta}{\hat{\phi}\omega}\big)\|x^{k+1}-x^{k}\|^{2}+\eta\omega(1-M^{2}\eta^{2})t_{k}^{2}\|d^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}2\omega r(r-2)\langle w^{k+1},x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\rangle+S_{k}\|w^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}t_{k}\big[\omega(\beta-2\rho)t_{k}+2\rho(\mu\omega+r\omega-\mu\eta)\big]\|w^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Substituting
\langle w^{k+1},x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\rangle\geq-\rho\|w^{k+1}\|^{2}
from the
\rho
-co-hypomonotonicity of
\Phi
into the last estimate, we obtain (
89
).

\square

F.5
The Proof of Lemma
14

From (
88
) and
\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\geq-\rho\|w^{k}\|^{2}
, we can show that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{L}_{k}&:=&\|r(x^{k}-x^{\star})+t_{k}(y^{k}-x^{k})\|^{2}+r\mu\|x^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}+\Lambda_{k}\|w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}2\big[\psi(t_{k}-r+1)-(r-1)\beta\big]\langle w^{k},r(x^{k}-x^{\star})+t_{k}(y^{k}-x^{k})\rangle\\
&&+{~}2\omega r(t_{k}-r+1)\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&\geq&\|r(x^{k}-x^{\star})+t_{k}(y^{k}-x^{k})+[\psi(t_{k}-r+1)-(r-1)\beta]w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\sigma_{k}\|w^{k}\|^{2}+r\mu\|x^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2},\end{array}

where
\sigma_{k}:=\Lambda_{k}-[\psi(t_{k}-r+1)-(r-1)\beta]^{2}-2\rho\omega r(t_{k}-r+1)
.
Since
\Lambda_{k}
is given by (
87
), we can simplify
\sigma_{k}
as
\sigma_{k}=\mu\big[\psi(t_{k}-r+1)-r\rho\big]^{2}-\mu\big[r^{2}\rho^{2}-\frac{(r-1)^{2}\beta(\eta-\beta)}{\mu+1}\big]
.
Substituting this value into the last estimate, we obtain (
90
).

Finally, the proof of (
91
) directly follows from the definition of
\mathcal{L}_{k}
in (
88
), the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and
y^{0}=x^{0}
.

\square

F.6
The Proof of Theorem
7.1

For simplicity of our presentation, we choose
\mu:=1
.
Since
\mu=1
and
\hat{\phi}:=\frac{1-L^{2}\eta^{2}}{2L^{2}\eta^{2}}>0
as in (
93
), we have
1-M^{2}\eta^{2}=\frac{1-L^{2}\eta^{2}}{2}
.
From this relation and the choice of
t_{0}
, we can derive from (
89
) that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{L}_{k}-\mathcal{L}_{k+1}&\geq&\big(2t_{k}-r-1-\frac{\eta}{\hat{\phi}\omega}\big)\|x^{k+1}-x^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{\eta\omega(1-L^{2}\eta^{2})t_{k}^{2}}{2}\|d^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}t_{k}\big[\omega(\beta-2\rho)t_{k}+2\rho(\omega+r\omega-\eta)\big]\|w^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{(r-2)}{2}\big[(\eta-\beta)^{2}t_{k}-\frac{2\Gamma}{r-2}-4\rho\omega r\big]\|w^{k+1}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Note that, since
t_{k+1}=t_{k}+1
from (
77
), we get
t_{k}=k+t_{0}
.
Summing up the last inequality from
k:=0
to
k:=K
, then taking the limit as
K\to\infty
and noting that
\mathcal{L}_{k}\geq 0
and
t_{k}=k+t_{0}
, we obtain (
94
).
Here, the last summable estimate of (
94
) follows from the fact that
\omega(\beta-2\rho)t_{k}+2\rho(\omega+r\omega-\eta)\geq\frac{\omega(\beta-2\rho)t_{k}}{2}
due to the choice of
t_{0}
in (
92
).

Finally,
\sigma_{k}
in Lemma
14
can be directly lower bounded by

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\sigma_{k}&\geq&\frac{(\eta-\beta)^{2}(t_{k}-r+1)^{2}}{8}=\frac{(\eta-\beta)^{2}(k+t_{0}-r+1)^{2}}{8}.\end{array}

Since
\sigma_{k}\|w^{k}\|^{2}\leq\mathcal{L}_{k}
from (
90
) and
\mathcal{L}_{k}\leq\mathcal{L}_{0}\leq\mathcal{R}_{0}^{2}
by induction and (
91
), combining these estimates, we obtain (
95
).

\square

F.7
The Proof of Theorem
7.2

From (
GAEG+
), (
76
), and (
77
), since
\mu=1
, we have

\begin{array}[]{lcl}y^{k+1}-x^{k+1}+\eta_{k}w^{k+1}&=&\theta_{k}(y^{k}-x^{k}+\eta_{k-1}w^{k})-(\eta\theta_{k}-\lambda_{k})d^{k}\\
&&-{~}(\theta_{k}\eta_{k-1}+\nu_{k}-\lambda_{k}\gamma_{k})w^{k}\\
&=&\theta_{k}(y^{k}-x^{k}+\eta_{k-1}w^{k})+\frac{\eta(r+1)}{t_{k+1}}d^{k}+\frac{s_{k}}{t_{k+1}}w^{k},\end{array}

where
s_{k}:=\frac{(r+4)(\eta-\beta)t_{k}-2(r+1)(\eta-\beta+\delta)}{2t_{k}}
.

Let us denote
v^{k}:=y^{k}-x^{k}+\eta_{k-1}w^{k}
.
Then, the last expression can be rewritten as follows:

\begin{array}[]{lcl}v^{k+1}&=&\theta_{k}v^{k}+(1-\theta_{k})\big[\frac{\eta(r+1)}{(1-\theta_{k})t_{k+1}}d^{k}+\frac{s_{k}}{(1-\theta_{k})t_{k+1}}w^{k}\big].\end{array}

Since
\theta_{k}\in(0,1)
, using convexity of
\|\cdot\|^{2}
, we have

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\|v^{k+1}\|^{2}&\leq&\theta_{k}\|v^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{1-\theta_{k}}\|\frac{\eta(r+1)}{t_{k+1}}d^{k}+\frac{s_{k}}{t_{k+1}}w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&\leq&\theta_{k}\|v^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{2\eta^{2}(r+1)^{2}}{(1-\theta_{k})t_{k+1}^{2}}\|d^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{2s_{k}^{2}}{(1-\theta_{k})t_{k+1}^{2}}\|w^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Noting that
s_{k}\leq\bar{s}:=\frac{(r+4)(\eta-\beta)+1}{2}
for any
t_{k}\geq 0
.
Multiplying both sides of the last expression by
t_{k+1}^{2}
and using this relation and
\theta_{k}=\frac{t_{k}-r-1}{t_{k+1}}
, we get

\hskip-4.30554pt\begin{array}[]{lcl}t_{k+1}^{2}\|v^{k+1}\|^{2}&\leq&t_{k}^{2}\|v^{k}\|^{2}-(rt_{k}+r+1)\|v^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{2\eta^{2}(r+1)^{2}t_{k+1}}{(r+2)}\|d^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{2\bar{s}^{2}t_{k+1}}{r+2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}\hskip-8.61108pt

(161)

Since
\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\frac{2\eta^{2}(r+1)^{2}t_{k+1}}{(r+2)}\|d^{k}\|^{2}<+\infty
and
\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\frac{2\bar{s}^{2}t_{k+1}}{r+2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}<+\infty
due to (
94
), the limit
\lim_{k\to\infty}t_{k}^{2}\|v^{k}\|^{2}
exists.
Moreover, since
\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}[(r-2)t_{k}+r-1]\|v^{k}\|^{2}<+\infty
, we can show that
\lim_{k\to\infty}t_{k}^{2}\|y^{k}-x^{k}-\eta_{k-1}w^{k}\|^{2}=0
.

Next, using again (
GAEG+
), (
76
), and (
77
) with
\mu=1
, we can also derive

\begin{array}[]{lcl}y^{k+1}-x^{k+1}&=&\theta_{k}(y^{k}-x^{k})-p^{k}-\frac{\eta(t_{k}-r-1)}{t_{k+1}}d^{k}\\
&=&\theta_{k}(y^{k}-x^{k})+\frac{r+1}{t_{k+1}}d^{k}-\frac{r(\eta-\beta)}{2t_{k+1}}w^{k+1}\\[4.30554pt]
&&-{~}\frac{[(\eta-\beta)t_{k}-(r-1)\eta]}{t_{k+1}}(w^{k+1}-w^{k})\\
&=&\theta_{k}(y^{k}-x^{k})+\frac{1}{t_{k+1}}\zeta_{k}-\frac{s_{k}}{t_{k+1}}(w^{k+1}-w^{k}),\end{array}

(162)

where
\zeta^{k}:=(r+1)d^{k}-\frac{r(\eta-\beta)}{2}w^{k+1}
and
s_{k}:=(\eta-\beta)t_{k}-(r-1)\eta
.

Utilizing
y^{k}-x^{k}=x^{k+1}-x^{k}+\eta d^{k}
, the last expression leads to

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\|y^{k+1}-x^{k+1}\|^{2}&=&\theta_{k}^{2}\|y^{k}-x^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{2\theta_{k}}{t_{k+1}}\langle\zeta^{k}-s_{k}(w^{k+1}-w^{k}),y^{k}-x^{k}\rangle\\
&&+{~}\frac{1}{t_{k+1}^{2}}\|\zeta^{k}-s_{k}(w^{k+1}-w^{k})\|^{2}\\
&=&\theta_{k}^{2}\|y^{k}-x^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{2\theta_{k}}{t_{k+1}}\langle\zeta^{k},y^{k}-x^{k}\rangle\\
&&-{~}\frac{2\theta_{k}s_{k}}{t_{k+1}}\langle w^{k+1}-w^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle-\frac{2\eta\theta_{k}s_{k}}{t_{k+1}}\langle w^{k+1}-w^{k},d^{k}\rangle\\
&&+{~}\frac{1}{t_{k+1}^{2}}\|\zeta^{k}-s_{k}(w^{k+1}-w^{k})\|^{2}.\end{array}

By
\langle w^{k+1}-w^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle\geq-\rho\|w^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}
from the
\rho
-co-hypomonotonicity of
\Phi
and Young’s inequality, we can prove that

\hskip-8.61108pt\begin{array}[]{lcl}\|y^{k+1}-x^{k+1}\|^{2}&\leq&\big(\theta_{k}^{2}+\frac{\theta_{k}}{t_{k+1}}\big)\|y^{k}-x^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{t_{k+1}^{2}}\big(2+t_{k+1}\theta_{k}\big)\|\zeta^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{(\eta+2\rho)t_{k+1}s_{k}\theta_{k}+2s_{k}^{2}}{t_{k+1}^{2}}\|w^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{\eta\theta_{k}s_{k}}{t_{k+1}}\|d^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}\hskip-17.22217pt

(163)

We can easily check that
\theta_{k}^{2}+\frac{\theta_{k}}{t_{k+1}}=\frac{t_{k}^{2}-(2r+1)t_{k}+r(r+1)}{t_{k+1}^{2}}
, and

\begin{array}[]{ccl}(\eta+2\rho)t_{k+1}s_{k}\theta_{k}+2s_{k}^{2}&\leq&(3\eta-2\beta+2\rho)(\eta-\beta)t_{k}^{2},\\[8.61108pt]
\eta\theta_{k}s_{k}&\leq&\eta(\eta-\beta)t_{k}^{2},\end{array}

where the first inequality holds if
t_{k}\geq\frac{(r^{2}-1)\eta+2(r-1)^{2}\eta^{2}}{(\eta+2\rho)[(\eta-\beta)(r+1)+(r-1)\eta]+4(r-1)\eta(\eta-\beta)}
and the second one requires
t_{k}\geq\frac{(r^{2}-1)\eta}{(r-1)\eta+(r+1)(\eta-\beta)}
, which holds as
t_{k}\to\infty
.

Multiplying (
163
) by
t_{k+1}^{2}
and using these bounds, we further obtain

\hskip-8.61108pt\begin{array}[]{lcl}t_{k+1}^{2}\|y^{k+1}-x^{k+1}\|^{2}&\leq&t_{k}^{2}\|y^{k}-x^{k}\|^{2}-[(2r+1)t_{k}-r(r+1)]\|y^{k}-x^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}(t_{k}-r+1)\|\zeta^{k}\|^{2}+\eta(\eta-\beta)t_{k}^{2}\|d^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}(3\eta-2\beta+2\rho)(\eta-\beta)t_{k}^{2}\|w^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}\hskip-17.22217pt

(164)

By Young’s inequality again, one has

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\|\zeta^{k}\|^{2}&\leq&2(r+1)^{2}\|d^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{r^{2}(\eta-\beta)^{2}}{2}\|w^{k+1}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Substituting this inequality into (
164
), we get

\hskip-8.61108pt\begin{array}[]{lcl}t_{k+1}^{2}\|y^{k+1}-x^{k+1}\|^{2}&\leq&t_{k}^{2}\|y^{k}-x^{k}\|^{2}-[(2r+1)t_{k}-r(r+1)]\|y^{k}-x^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\big[\eta(\eta-\beta)t_{k}^{2}+2(r+1)^{2}(t_{k}-r+1)\big]\|d^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}(3\eta-2\beta+2\rho)(\eta-\beta)t_{k}^{2}\|w^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{r^{2}(\eta-\beta)^{2}}{2}(t_{k}-r+1)\|w^{k+1}\|^{2}.\end{array}\hskip-17.22217pt

Note that the last three terms are summable due to (
94
).
This implies that
\lim_{k\to\infty}t_{k}^{2}\|y^{k}-x^{k}\|^{2}=0
as before, which proves the last limit in (
96
).

Now, utilizing Young’s inequality, one can show that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}[(\eta-\beta)t_{k-1}-\delta]^{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}&=&t_{k}^{2}\eta_{k-1}^{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&\leq&2t_{k}^{2}\|y^{k}-x^{k}+\eta_{k-1}w^{k}\|^{2}+2t_{k}^{2}\|y^{k}-x^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Combining this inequality,
\lim_{k\to\infty}t_{k}^{2}\|y^{k}-x^{k}-\eta_{k-1}w^{k}\|^{2}=0
, and
\lim_{k\to\infty}t_{k}^{2}\|y^{k}-x^{k}\|^{2}=0
, we get
\lim_{k\to\infty}[(\eta-\beta)t_{k-1}-\delta]^{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}=0
, which also implies
\lim_{k\to\infty}t_{k}^{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}=0
.
This proves the first line of (
96
).

Finally, using Young’s inequality one more time, we get

\begin{array}[]{lcl}t_{k}^{2}\|x^{k+1}-x^{k}\|^{2}=t_{k}^{2}\|y^{k}-x^{k}-\eta d^{k}\|^{2}\leq 2t_{k}^{2}\|y^{k}-x^{k}\|^{2}+2\eta^{2}t_{k}^{2}\|d^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Since
\lim_{k\to\infty}t_{k}^{2}\|y^{k}-x^{k}\|^{2}=0
and
\lim_{k\to\infty}t_{k}^{2}\|d^{k}\|^{2}=0
, this inequality implies
\lim_{k\to\infty}t_{k}^{2}\|x^{k+1}-x^{k}\|^{2}=0
, which leads to the second line of (
96
).

\square

F.8
The Proof of Theorem
7.3

From the proof of Theorem
7.1
, we have
r\|x^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\leq\mathcal{L}_{k}\leq\mathcal{L}_{0}\leq\mathcal{R}_{0}^{2}
, we conclude that
\{x^{k}\}
is bounded, and hence, it has a cluster point.
Let
x^{*}
be a cluster point of
\{x^{k}\}
and
\{x^{k_{i}}\}
be a subsequence converging to
x^{*}
.

Consider the subsequence
\{z^{k_{i}}\}
with
z^{k_{i}}:=Fx^{k_{i}}+\xi^{k_{i}}
for
\xi^{k_{i}}\in Tx^{k_{i}}
.
By (
96
), we have
\lim_{i\to\infty}\|Fx^{k_{i}}+\xi^{k_{i}}\|^{2}=\lim_{i\to\infty}\|z^{k_{i}}\|^{2}=0
, implies that
z^{k_{i}}\to 0
.
Since
\mathrm{gra}(T)
is closed and
F
is
L
-Lipschitz continuous,
\mathrm{gra}(\Phi)=\mathrm{gra}(F+T)
is also closed and
(x^{k_{i}},z^{k_{i}})\in\mathrm{gra}(\Phi)
converges to
(x^{\star},0)
.
By the closedness of
\mathrm{gra}(\Phi)
, we have
(x^{*},0)\in\mathrm{gra}(\Phi)
, which means that
0\in\Phi{x^{*}}=Fx^{*}+Tx^{*}
.

Next, we prove that
\lim_{k\to\infty}\|x^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}
exists.
By the
\rho
-co-hypomonotonicity of
\Phi
, the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, and
r\|x^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\leq\mathcal{R}_{0}^{2}
, we have

\begin{array}[]{lcl}-\rho t_{k}\|w^{k}\|^{2}\leq t_{k}\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\leq t_{k}\|w^{k}\|\|x^{k}-x^{\star}\|\leq\frac{\mathcal{R}_{0}}{\sqrt{r}}t_{k}\|w^{k}\|.\end{array}

Note that
\lim_{k\to\infty}t_{k}\|w^{k}\|=0
due to the first line of (
96
), and
\lim_{k\to\infty}t_{k}\|w^{k}\|^{2}=0
due to the third summable expression in (
94
), we conclude that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\lim_{k\to\infty}|t_{k}\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle|=0.\end{array}

(165)

From (
76
) and (
77
), with
z^{k}=w^{k}
, we have

\begin{array}[]{lcl}w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}&=&w^{k+1}-w^{k}-d^{k}+\frac{r-1}{t_{k}}w^{k},\\
