t_{k+1}p^{k}&=&[(\eta-\beta)t_{k}-\delta](w^{k+1}-w^{k})-\eta t_{k}d^{k}+[\eta(r-1)-\delta]w^{k}.\end{array}

Applying Young’s inequality, these relations lead to

\begin{array}[]{lclcl}0&\leq&t_{k}^{2}\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}&\leq&3t_{k}^{2}\|w^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}+3t_{k}^{2}\|d^{k}\|^{2}+3(r-1)^{2}t_{k}\|w^{k}\|^{2},\\
0&\leq&t_{k+1}^{2}\|p^{k}\|^{2}&\leq&3[(\eta-\beta)t_{k}-\delta]^{2}\|w^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}+3\eta^{2}t_{k}^{2}\|d^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&&&+{~}3[\eta(r-1)-\delta]^{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Each term on the right-hand side of each inequality is summable, we obtain

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\sum_{k=0}^{+\infty}t_{k}^{2}\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}<+\infty,\\
\sum_{k=0}^{+\infty}t_{k+1}^{2}\|p^{k}\|^{2}<+\infty.\end{array}

(166)

Let us define

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{Q}_{k}&:=&\|r(x^{k}-x^{\star})+t_{k}(y^{k}-x^{k})\|^{2}+r\|x^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}2r[(\eta-\beta)t_{k}-\eta(r-1)]\big[\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle+\rho\|w^{k}\|^{2}].\end{array}

(167)

Then, similar to the proof of (
156
) in Lemma
10
, with
\mu=1
, we can show that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{Q}_{k}-\mathcal{Q}_{k+1}&=&(2t_{k}-r-1)\|x^{k+1}-x^{k}\|^{2}+\eta^{2}t_{k}^{2}\|d^{k}\|^{2}-t_{k+1}^{2}\|p^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}2r[\eta(r-2)-\delta]\langle w^{k+1},x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&&+{~}2\big[((\eta-\beta)t_{k}-\delta)(t_{k}-r-1)+\eta t_{k}\big]\langle w^{k+1}-w^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle\\
&&-{~}2\big[((\eta-\beta)r+\delta)t_{k}-\eta(r-1)t_{k}-\delta(r+1)\big]\langle w^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle\\
&&+{~}2\eta t_{k}\langle\hat{w}^{k+1}-w^{k+1},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle\\
&&+{~}2\rho r[(\eta-\beta)t_{k}-\eta(r-1)]\|w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}2\rho r[(\eta-\beta)t_{k+1}-\eta(r-1)]\|w^{k+1}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Next, by the
\rho
-co-hypomonotonicity of
\Phi
and Young’s inequality, we have

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\langle w^{k+1}-w^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle\geq-\rho\|w^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2},\\
2\eta t_{k}|\langle\hat{w}^{k+1}-w^{k+1},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle|\geq-\eta t_{k}^{2}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}-w^{k+1}\|^{2}-\eta\|x^{k+1}-x^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Substituting these inequalities into
\mathcal{Q}_{k}-\mathcal{Q}_{k+1}
above, one can derive that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{Q}_{k+1}-\mathcal{Q}_{k}&\leq&t_{k+1}^{2}\|p^{k}\|^{2}-\eta^{2}t_{k}^{2}\|d^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}2r[\eta(r-2)-\delta]|\langle w^{k+1},x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\rangle|\\
&&+{~}2\rho\big[((\eta-\beta)t_{k}-\delta)(t_{k}-r-1)+\eta t_{k}\big]\|w^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\big[(\eta-\beta r+\delta)t_{k}-\delta(r+1)\big]\|w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\big[(\eta-\beta r+\delta-2)t_{k}-(\delta-1)(r+1)\big]\|x^{k+1}-x^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}2\rho r[(\eta-\beta)t_{k+1}-\eta(r-1)]\|w^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\eta t_{k}^{2}\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}+\eta\|x^{k+1}-x^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

From (
94
), (
165
), and (
166
), we can see that all the terms on the right-hand side of this inequality are summable and
\mathcal{Q}_{k}\geq 0
.
We conclude that
\lim_{k\to\infty}\mathcal{Q}_{k}
exists due to
(
9
, Proposition 5.31)
.

By (
167
), the existence of
\lim_{k\to\infty}\mathcal{Q}_{k}
, and (
165
), we conclude that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\lim_{k\to\infty}\big[\|r(x^{k}-x^{\star})+t_{k}(y^{k}-x^{k})\|^{2}+r\|x^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\big]\ \text{exists}.\end{array}

(168)

Now, since
\|x^{k}-x^{\star}\|\leq\frac{\mathcal{R}_{0}}{\sqrt{r}}
and
\lim_{k\to\infty}t_{k}\|y^{k}-x^{k}\|=0
due to (
96
), we get

\begin{array}[]{lcl}|t_{k}\langle x^{k}-x^{\star},y^{k}-x^{k}\rangle|\leq t_{k}\|x^{k}-x^{\star}\|\|y^{k}-x^{k}\|\leq\frac{\mathcal{R}_{0}}{\sqrt{r}}t_{k}\|y^{k}-x^{k}\|\to 0,~k\to\infty.\end{array}

As a consequence, we conclude that
\lim_{k\to\infty}t_{k}\langle x^{k}-x^{\star},y^{k}-x^{k}\rangle=0
.
Now, we consider

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\bar{\mathcal{T}}_{[2]}&:=&\|r(x^{k}-x^{\star})+t_{k}(y^{k}-x^{k})\|^{2}+r\|x^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\\
&=&(r^{2}+r)\|x^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}+2rt_{k}\langle x^{k}-x^{\star},y^{k}-x^{k}\rangle+t_{k}^{2}\|y^{k}-x^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

The limit on the left-hand side exists due to (
168
), while the limits of the last two terms
t_{k}\langle x^{k}-x^{\star},y^{k}-x^{k}\rangle
and
t_{k}^{2}\|y^{k}-x^{k}\|^{2}
on the right hand side are zero, we conclude that
\lim_{k\to\infty}\|x^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}
exists.

Finally, since any cluster point of
\{x^{k}\}
is in
\mathrm{zer}(\Phi)
, we conclude that
\{x^{k}\}
converges to
x^{\star}\in\mathrm{zer}(\Phi)
.
Combining the convergence of
\{x^{k}\}
and
\lim_{k\to\infty}\|x^{k}-y^{k}\|=0
, we can say that
\{y^{k}\}
also converges to
x^{\star}\in\mathrm{zer}(\Phi)
due to the well-known Opial’s lemma.

\square

F.9
The Proof of Lemma
15

Denote
\hat{a}_{k}:=\omega(t_{k}+1-r-\mu)+\mu\eta
.
First, since
\omega=\frac{\mu(\eta-\beta)}{\mu+1}
and
\eta\geq\frac{(r+\mu-1)\beta}{r-2}
, we have
\hat{a}_{k}\leq\omega t_{k}
.
Similarly, since
r>2
, we can show that
\eta\geq\frac{(r+\mu-1)\beta}{r-2}\geq\frac{(r+\mu)\beta}{r-1}
.
Thus we also get
a_{k}\leq\omega t_{k}^{2}
.

Next, utilizing (
GAEG+
), we can express
\mathcal{F}_{k}
from (
97
) as

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{F}_{k}&=&2a_{k}\langle w^{k+1}-w^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle+2a_{k}\langle z^{k+1}-w^{k+1},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle\\
&&-{~}2a_{k}\langle z^{k}-w^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle\\
&=&2a_{k}\langle w^{k+1}-w^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle+2a_{k+1}\langle z^{k+1}-w^{k+1},\theta_{k}(x^{k+1}-x^{k})\rangle\\
&&+{~}2(a_{k}-a_{k+1}\theta_{k})\langle z^{k+1}-w^{k+1},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle\\
&&-{~}2a_{k}\langle z^{k}-w^{k},y^{k}-x^{k}-\eta d^{k}\rangle\\
&=&2a_{k}\langle w^{k+1}-w^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle+2a_{k+1}\langle z^{k+1}-w^{k+1},y^{k+1}-x^{k+1}\rangle\\
&&-{~}2a_{k}\langle z^{k}-w^{k},y^{k}-x^{k}\rangle+2(a_{k}-a_{k+1}\theta_{k})\langle z^{k+1}-w^{k+1},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle\\
&&+{~}2a_{k+1}\langle z^{k+1}-w^{k+1},p^{k}\rangle+2\eta a_{k}\langle z^{k}-w^{k},d^{k}\rangle.\end{array}

By the definition of
p^{k}
from (
76
), we have

\begin{array}[]{lcl}t_{k+1}p^{k}&=&t_{k+1}\eta_{k}z^{k+1}-t_{k+1}\lambda_{k}\hat{w}^{k+1}+t_{k+1}\nu_{k}z^{k}\\
&=&[(\eta-\beta)t_{k}-\delta]z^{k+1}-\eta t_{k}\hat{w}^{k+1}+\beta t_{k}z^{k}\\
&=&\eta t_{k}(z^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1})-\beta t_{k}(z^{k+1}-z^{k})-\delta z^{k+1}.\end{array}

Therefore, applying Young’s inequality, for any
\Delta>0
, we can derive that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\hat{\mathcal{T}}_{[1]}&:=&a_{k+1}\langle z^{k+1}-w^{k+1},p^{k}\rangle=\hat{a}_{k}\langle z^{k+1}-w^{k+1},t_{k+1}p^{k}\rangle\\
&=&\hat{a}_{k}\langle\eta t_{k}(z^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1})-\beta t_{k}(z^{k+1}-z^{k})-\delta z^{k+1},z^{k+1}-w^{k+1}\rangle\\
&\geq&-\hat{a}_{k}[(\eta+\beta)t_{k}+\delta]\|z^{k+1}-w^{k+1}\|^{2}-\frac{\eta\hat{a}_{k}t_{k}}{4}\|z^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}\frac{\beta\hat{a}_{k}t_{k}}{4}\|z^{k+1}-z^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{\delta\hat{a}_{k}}{4}\|z^{k+1}\|^{2},\\
\hat{\mathcal{T}}_{[2]}&:=&\eta a_{k}\langle z^{k}-w^{k},d^{k}\rangle\geq-\frac{\Delta\eta ra_{k}}{2}\|z^{k}-w^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{\eta a_{k}}{2\Delta r}\|d^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Substituting these two inequalities of
\hat{\mathcal{T}}_{[1]}
and
\hat{\mathcal{T}}_{[2]}
into
\mathcal{F}_{k}
above, we get

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{F}_{k}&\geq&2a_{k+1}\langle z^{k+1}-w^{k+1},y^{k+1}-x^{k+1}\rangle-2a_{k}\langle z^{k}-w^{k},y^{k}-x^{k}\rangle\\
&&-{~}2\hat{a}_{k}[(\eta+\beta)t_{k}+\delta]\|z^{k+1}-w^{k+1}\|^{2}-\frac{\eta\hat{a}_{k}t_{k}}{2}\|z^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}\frac{\beta\hat{a}_{k}t_{k}}{2}\|z^{k+1}-z^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{\delta\hat{a}_{k}}{2}\|z^{k+1}\|^{2}-\frac{\eta a_{k}}{\Delta r}\|d^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}\Delta\eta ra_{k}\|z^{k}-w^{k}\|^{2}+2a_{k}\langle w^{k+1}-w^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle\\
&&+{~}2(a_{k}-a_{k+1}\theta_{k})\langle z^{k+1}-w^{k+1},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle.\end{array}

Finally, substituting
b_{k}:=a_{k}-a_{k+1}\theta_{k}=\omega(r+\mu-1)(t_{k}-r-\mu)+\mu\eta(r+\mu)
,
\hat{a}_{k}\leq\omega t_{k}
, and
a_{k}\leq\omega t_{k}^{2}
into the last inequality, we can further lower bound it and obtain (
98
).

\square

F.10
The Proof of Lemma
16

First, substituting
\mathcal{F}_{k}
from (
97
) and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{E}_{k}}
from (
86
) into (
84
), and choosing
c_{1}:=1
in
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{E}_{k}}
, we get

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{P}_{k}-\mathcal{P}_{k+1}&\geq&\big(S_{k}-\frac{\delta\omega t_{k}}{2}\big)\|z^{k+1}\|^{2}+\mu(2t_{k}-r-\mu)\|x^{k+1}-x^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\Lambda_{k+1}\|z^{k+1}\|^{2}-\Lambda_{k}\|z^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{\beta\omega t_{k}^{2}}{2}\|z^{k+1}-z^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\eta\omega\big(1-\frac{1}{\Delta r}-M^{2}\eta^{2}\big)t_{k}^{2}\|d^{k}\|^{2}+\eta\omega\hat{\phi}t_{k}^{2}\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\eta\omega\big(\phi-\frac{1}{2}\big)t_{k}^{2}\|z^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}-\Delta r\eta\omega t_{k}^{2}\|z^{k}-w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}2\omega t_{k}\big[(\eta(2+\phi)+\beta)t_{k}+\delta\big]\|z^{k+1}-w^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}2r[(\eta-\beta)t_{k+1}-\eta(r-1)]\langle z^{k+1},x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&&-{~}2r[(\eta-\beta)t_{k}-\eta(r-1)]\langle z^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&&+{~}2a_{k+1}\langle z^{k+1}-w^{k+1},y^{k+1}-x^{k+1}\rangle-2a_{k}\langle z^{k}-w^{k},y^{k}-x^{k}\rangle\\
&&+{~}2b_{k}\langle z^{k+1}-w^{k+1},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle+2\mu\eta t_{k}\langle\hat{w}^{k+1}-z^{k+1},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle\\
&&+{~}2a_{k}\langle w^{k+1}-w^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle+2\omega r(r-2)\langle z^{k+1},x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\rangle.\end{array}

Next, applying Young’s inequality, we have

\begin{array}[]{lcl}2\mu\eta t_{k}\langle\hat{w}^{k+1}-z^{k+1},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle&\geq&-\frac{2\mu^{2}\eta}{\omega}\|x^{k+1}-x^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{\eta\omega t_{k}^{2}}{2}\|z^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Since
\eta\geq\frac{(r+\mu-1)\beta}{r-2}
, we have
b_{k}\leq(r+\mu-1)\omega t_{k}
from Lemma
15
.
Applying Young’s inequality again, and using
b_{k}\leq(r+\mu-1)\omega t_{k}
, we can show that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\hat{\mathcal{T}}_{[3]}&:=&2b_{k}\langle z^{k+1}-w^{k+1},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle\\
&\geq&-\frac{b_{k}}{\omega(r+\mu-1)}\|x^{k+1}-x^{k}\|^{2}-\omega(r+\mu-1)b_{k}\|z^{k+1}-w^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&\geq&t_{k}\|x^{k+1}-x^{k}\|^{2}-\omega^{2}(r+\mu-1)^{2}t_{k}\|z^{k+1}-w^{k+1}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Substituting the last two inequalities into the first estimate, we obtain

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{P}_{k}-\mathcal{P}_{k+1}&\geq&\big(S_{k}-\frac{\delta\omega t_{k}}{2}\big)\|z^{k+1}\|^{2}+\mu\big(t_{k}-r-\mu-\frac{2\mu\eta}{\omega}\big)\|x^{k+1}-x^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\Lambda_{k+1}\|z^{k+1}\|^{2}-\Lambda_{k}\|z^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{\beta\omega t_{k}^{2}}{2}\|z^{k+1}-z^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\eta\omega\big(1-\frac{1}{\Delta r}-M^{2}\eta^{2}\big)t_{k}^{2}\|d^{k}\|^{2}+\eta\omega\hat{\phi}t_{k}^{2}\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\eta\omega(\phi-1)t_{k}^{2}\|z^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}-\Delta r\eta\omega t_{k}^{2}\|z^{k}-w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}\omega t_{k}\big[2(\eta(2+\phi)+\beta)t_{k}+2\delta+\omega(r+\mu-1)^{2}\big]\|z^{k+1}-w^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}2r[(\eta-\beta)t_{k+1}-\eta(r-1)]\langle z^{k+1},x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&&-{~}2r[(\eta-\beta)t_{k}-\eta(r-1)]\langle z^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&&+{~}2a_{k+1}\langle z^{k+1}-w^{k+1},y^{k+1}-x^{k+1}\rangle-2a_{k}\langle z^{k}-w^{k},y^{k}-x^{k}\rangle\\
&&+{~}2\omega r(r-2)\langle z^{k+1},x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\rangle+2a_{k}\langle w^{k+1}-w^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle.\end{array}

Utilizing
\mathcal{G}_{k}
from (
99
), the last inequality leads to

\hskip-12.91663pt\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{G}_{k}-\mathcal{G}_{k+1}&\geq&\big(S_{k}-\frac{\delta\omega t_{k}}{2}\big)\|z^{k+1}\|^{2}+\mu\big(t_{k}-r-\mu-\frac{2\mu\eta}{\omega}\big)\|x^{k+1}-x^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\eta\omega\big(1-\frac{1}{\Delta r}-M^{2}\eta^{2}\big)t_{k}^{2}\|d^{k}\|^{2}+\eta\omega\hat{\phi}t_{k}^{2}\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\eta\omega(\phi-1)t_{k}^{2}\|z^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}-\Delta r\eta\omega t_{k}^{2}\|z^{k}-w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}2\omega r(r-2)\langle z^{k+1},x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\rangle+2a_{k}\langle w^{k+1}-w^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle\\
&&-{~}\omega t_{k}\big[2(\eta(2+\phi)+\beta)t_{k}+2\delta+\omega(r+\mu-1)^{2}\big]\|z^{k+1}-w^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{\beta\omega t_{k}^{2}}{2}\|z^{k+1}-z^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}\hskip-25.83325pt

(169)

Now, applying again Young’s inequality, the
\rho
-co-hypomonotonicity of
\Phi
, and
a_{k}\leq\omega t_{k}^{2}
, we can prove that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\hat{\mathcal{T}}_{[4]}&:=&2a_{k}\langle w^{k+1}-w^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle\\
&\geq&2a_{k}\big[\langle w^{k+1}-w^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle+\rho\|w^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}\big]\\
&&-{~}4\rho a_{k}\|z^{k+1}-z^{k}\|^{2}-8\rho a_{k}\|z^{k+1}-w^{k+1}\|^{2}-8\rho a_{k}\|z^{k}-w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&\geq&-4\rho\omega t_{k}^{2}\|z^{k+1}-z^{k}\|^{2}-8\rho\omega t_{k}^{2}\|z^{k+1}-w^{k+1}\|^{2}-8\rho\omega t_{k}^{2}\|z^{k}-w^{k}\|^{2},\\
\hat{\mathcal{T}}_{[5]}&:=&2\omega r(r-2)\langle z^{k+1},x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&=&2\omega r(r-2)\langle w^{k+1},x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\rangle+2\omega r(r-2)\langle z^{k+1}-w^{k+1},x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&\geq&-2\rho\omega r(r-2)\|w^{k+1}\|^{2}-\eta\omega t_{k}^{2}\|z^{k+1}-w^{k+1}\|^{2}-\frac{\omega r^{2}(r-2)^{2}}{\eta t_{k}^{2}}\|x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\\
&\geq&-4\rho\omega r(r-2)\|z^{k+1}\|^{2}-(\eta\omega t_{k}^{2}+4\rho\omega r(r-2))\|z^{k+1}-w^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}\frac{\omega r^{2}(r-2)^{2}}{\eta t_{k}^{2}}\|x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\|.\end{array}

Substituting
\hat{\mathcal{T}}_{[4]}
and
\hat{\mathcal{T}}_{[5]}
into the expression
\mathcal{G}_{k}-\mathcal{G}_{k+1}
, we can further derive

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{G}_{k}-\mathcal{G}_{k+1}&\geq&\big[S_{k}-\frac{\delta\omega t_{k}}{2}-4\rho\omega r(r-2)\big]\|z^{k+1}\|^{2}-\frac{\omega r^{2}(r-2)^{2}}{\eta t_{k}^{2}}\|x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\eta\omega\big(1-\frac{1}{\Delta r}-M^{2}\eta^{2}\big)t_{k}^{2}\|d^{k}\|^{2}+\eta\omega\hat{\phi}t_{k}^{2}\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\eta\omega(\phi-1)t_{k}^{2}\|z^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}-\big(\Delta r\eta+8\rho\big)\omega t_{k}^{2}\|z^{k}-w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}\hat{\Theta}_{k}\|z^{k+1}-w^{k+1}\|^{2}+\frac{(\beta-8\rho)\omega t_{k}^{2}}{2}\|z^{k+1}-z^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\mu\big(t_{k}-r-\mu-\frac{2\mu\eta}{\omega}\big)\|x^{k+1}-x^{k}\|^{2},\end{array}

where
\hat{\Theta}_{k}:=\omega t_{k}\big[(\eta(5+2\phi)+2\beta+8\rho)t_{k}+2\delta+\omega(r+\mu-1)^{2}\big]+4\rho\omega r(r-2)
.
This exactly proves (
100
).

\square

F.11
The Proof of Lemma
17

Choosing
\phi:=1
in (
100
), and
\beta:=8\rho+2\epsilon
, (
100
) reduces to

\hskip-4.30554pt\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{G}_{k}-\mathcal{G}_{k+1}&\geq&\big[S_{k}-\frac{\delta\omega t_{k}}{2}-4\rho\omega r(r-2)\big]\|z^{k+1}\|^{2}-\frac{\omega r^{2}(r-2)^{2}}{\eta t_{k}^{2}}\|x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\eta\omega\big(1-\frac{1}{\Delta r}-M^{2}\eta^{2}\big)t_{k}^{2}\|d^{k}\|^{2}-\big(\Delta r\eta+8\rho\big)\omega t_{k}^{2}\|z^{k}-w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\big(\Delta r\eta+8\rho\big)\omega t_{k+1}^{2}\|z^{k+1}-w^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\mu\big(t_{k}-r-\mu-\frac{2\mu\eta}{\omega}\big)\|x^{k+1}-x^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\eta\omega\hat{\phi}t_{k}^{2}\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}-A_{k}\|z^{k+1}-w^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\hat{\epsilon}\omega\eta t_{k+1}^{2}\|z^{k+1}-w^{k+1}\|^{2}+\epsilon\omega t_{k}^{2}\|z^{k+1}-z^{k}\|^{2},\end{array}\hskip-17.22217pt

(170)

where
A_{k}:=\Theta_{k}+\big(\Delta r\eta+8\rho+\hat{\epsilon}\eta\big)\omega t_{k+1}^{2}
for some
\hat{\epsilon}\geq 0
.

Now, since
\beta=8\rho+2\epsilon\geq 8\rho
,
\phi=1
, and
\eta\geq\frac{(r+\mu-1)\beta}{r-2}
, we can show that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}A_{k}&=&\omega t_{k}\big[(\eta(5+2\phi)+2\beta+8\rho)t_{k}+2\delta+\omega(r+\mu-1)^{2}\big]+4\rho\omega r(r-2)\\
&&+{~}\omega(\Delta r\eta+8\rho+\hat{\epsilon}\eta)(t_{k}^{2}+2t_{k}+1)\\
&\leq&\big[8+\frac{4(r-2)}{r+\mu-1}+\Delta r+\hat{\epsilon}\big]\eta\omega t_{k}^{2}\\
&\leq&\eta\omega\Theta t_{k}^{2},\end{array}

where
\Theta:=12+\Delta r+\hat{\epsilon}
,
provided that
t_{k}\geq\frac{D+\sqrt{D^{2}+4\eta E}}{2\eta}
with
D:=2\delta+\omega(r+\mu-1)^{2}+2\Delta r\eta+2\beta+2\hat{\epsilon}\eta
and
E:=4\rho r(r-2)+\Delta r\eta+8\rho+\hat{\epsilon}\eta
.

We also have
S_{k}-\frac{\delta\omega t_{k}}{2}-4\rho\omega r(r-2)=\frac{\omega[3(r-2)\psi-(r-1)\beta]t_{k}-\hat{\Gamma}}{2}
, where
\hat{\Gamma}:=2\Gamma+8\rho\omega r(r-2)
.

Moreover, from (
78
), and
z^{k+1}:=u^{k+1}+\xi^{k+1}
in (
75
), we can write

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\|z^{k+1}-w^{k+1}\|^{2}&\leq&\kappa\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}+\hat{\kappa}\|d^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Substituting the last three expressions into (
170
), rearranging the result, and using (
101
), we can prove that

\hskip-8.61108pt\begin{array}[]{lcl}\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{k}-\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{k+1}&\geq&\frac{\omega[3(r-2)\psi-(r-1)\beta]t_{k}-\hat{\Gamma}}{2}\|z^{k+1}\|^{2}-\frac{\omega r^{2}(r-2)^{2}}{\eta t_{k}^{2}}\|x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\eta\omega(\hat{\phi}-\kappa\Theta)t_{k}^{2}\|w^{k+1}-\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\mu\big(t_{k}-r-\mu-\frac{2\mu\eta}{\omega}\big)\|x^{k+1}-x^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\eta\omega\big(1-\frac{1}{\Delta r}-\hat{\kappa}\Theta-M^{2}\eta^{2}\big)t_{k}^{2}\|d^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\hat{\epsilon}\omega\eta t_{k+1}^{2}\|z^{k+1}-w^{k+1}\|^{2}+\epsilon\omega t_{k}^{2}\|z^{k+1}-z^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}\hskip-8.61108pt

This is exactly (
103
).

\square

F.12
The Proof of Lemma
18

First, we can easily show that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\tilde{\mathcal{T}}_{[1]}&:=&\|r(x^{k}-x^{\star})+t_{k}(y^{k}-x^{k})\|^{2}+\mu r\|x^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\\
&=&(r^{2}+r\mu)\|x^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}+2rt_{k}\langle x^{k}-x^{\star},y^{k}-x^{k}\rangle+t_{k}^{2}\|y^{k}-x^{k}\|^{2}\\
&=&r\big(\mu-\frac{1}{2}\big)\|x^{k}{\!}-{\!}x^{\star}\|^{2}+\frac{2r+1}{2r}\|r(x^{k}{\!}-{\!}x^{\star})+\frac{2rt_{k}}{2r+1}(y^{k}{\!}-{\!}x^{k})\|^{2}+\frac{t_{k}^{2}}{2r+1}\|y^{k}{\!}-{\!}x^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Denote
s_{k}:=(\eta-\beta)t_{k}-\eta(r-1)
.
Then, substituting
\tilde{\mathcal{T}}_{[1]}
into (
101
), and using Young’s inequality and
a_{k}\leq\omega t_{k}^{2}
, we can derive that

\hskip-4.30554pt\begin{array}[]{lcl}\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{k}&:=&\|r(x^{k}-x^{\star})+t_{k}(y^{k}-x^{k})\|^{2}+r\mu\|x^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}+\Lambda_{k}\|z^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}2rs_{k}\langle z^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle+2c_{k}\langle z^{k},y^{k}-x^{k}\rangle\\
&&+{~}2a_{k}\langle z^{k}-w^{k},y^{k}-x^{k}\rangle+\alpha_{k}\|z^{k}-w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&\geq&r\big(\mu-\frac{1}{2}\big)\|x^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}+\frac{t_{k}^{2}}{2r+1}\|y^{k}-x^{k}\|^{2}+\Lambda_{k}\|z^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{2r+1}{2r}\|r(x^{k}-x^{\star})+\frac{2rt_{k}}{2r+1}(y^{k}-x^{k})\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{(2r+1)c_{k}}{rt_{k}}\langle z^{k},r(x^{k}-x^{\star})+\frac{2rt_{k}}{2r+1}(y^{k}-x^{k})\rangle\\
&&+{~}\big[2rs_{k}-\frac{(2r+1)c_{k}}{t_{k}}\big]\langle z^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&&-{~}\frac{a_{k}}{(2r+1)\omega}\|y^{k}-x^{k}\|^{2}+\big[\alpha_{k}-(2r+1)\omega a_{k}\big]\|z^{k}-w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&=&r\big(\mu-\frac{1}{2}\big)\|x^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}+\big[\Lambda_{k}-\frac{(2r+1)c_{k}^{2}}{2rt_{k}^{2}}\big]\|z^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{2r+1}{2r}\|r(x^{k}-x^{\star})+\frac{2rt_{k}}{2r+1}(y^{k}-x^{k})+\frac{c_{k}}{t_{k}}z^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\big[2rs_{k}-\frac{(2r+1)c_{k}}{t_{k}}\big]\langle z^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&&+{~}\big[\alpha_{k}-(2r+1)\omega a_{k}\big]\|z^{k}-w^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}\hskip-8.61108pt

(171)

We note that if
t_{k}\geq\frac{\mu}{\omega}(r-1)[\eta-(2r+1)\omega]
, then

\hskip-4.30554pt\begin{array}[]{lcl}n_{k}&:=&2rs_{k}-\frac{(2r+1)c_{k}}{t_{k}}\leq 2r\omega t_{k}.\end{array}\hskip-17.22217pt

(172)

By Young’s inequality and
\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\geq-\rho\|w^{k}\|^{2}
, we have

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\tilde{\mathcal{T}}_{[2]}&:=&n_{k}\langle z^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&=&n_{k}\langle z^{k}-w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle+n_{k}\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&\geq&-n_{k}\big(\frac{n_{k}}{2r}+2\rho)\|z^{k}-w^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{r}{2}\|x^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}-2\rho n_{k}\|z^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Substituting this inequality
\tilde{\mathcal{T}}_{[2]}
into (
171
), we can show that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{k}&\geq&r(\mu-1)\|x^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}+\big[\Lambda_{k}-\frac{(2r+1)c_{k}^{2}}{2rt_{k}^{2}}-2\rho n_{k}\big]\|z^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{2r+1}{2r}\|r(x^{k}-x^{\star})+\frac{2rt_{k}}{2r+1}(y^{k}-x^{k})+\frac{c_{k}}{t_{k}}z^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\big[\alpha_{k}-(2r+1)\omega a_{k}-\frac{n_{k}^{2}}{2r}-2\rho n_{k}\big]\|z^{k}-w^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

(173)

One the one hand, we can lower bound

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\tilde{\mathcal{T}}_{[3]}&:=&\Lambda_{k}-\frac{(2r+1)c_{k}^{2}}{2rt_{k}^{2}}-2\rho n_{k}\geq\frac{(\eta-\beta)^{2}t_{k}^{2}}{2(\mu+1)^{2}}.\end{array}

provided that
\mu\geq 1
,
r>2
, and
t_{k}\geq\hat{t}_{0}
, where
\hat{t}_{0}
is defined as in (
105
).

On the other hand, using (
172
) and
a_{k}\leq\omega t_{k}
, we can show that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\tilde{\mathcal{T}}_{[4]}&:=&\alpha_{k}-(2r+1)\omega a_{k}-\frac{n_{k}^{2}}{2r}-2\rho n_{k}\geq\big[\frac{\Delta r\eta}{\omega}-4r-1\big]\omega^{2}t_{k}^{2},\end{array}

due to
t_{k}\geq r>\frac{r}{2}
.
Substituting
\tilde{\mathcal{T}}_{[3]}
and
\tilde{\mathcal{T}}_{[4]}
into (
173
), we obtain (
104
).

Finally, if we choose
\Delta:=3\geq\frac{(\eta-\beta)[1+2(4r+1)\mu]}{2r\eta\mu(\mu+1)}
, then we have
\big[\frac{\Delta r\eta}{\omega}-4r-1\big]\omega^{2}\geq\frac{(\eta-\beta)^{2}}{2(\mu+1)^{2}}
.
Consequently, (
104
) leads to

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{k}&\geq&r(\mu-1)\|x^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}+\frac{(\eta-\beta)^{2}t_{k}^{2}}{2(\mu+1)^{2}}\big[\|z^{k}\|^{2}+\|z^{k}-w^{k}\|^{2}\big]\\
&\geq&r(\mu-1)\|x^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}+\frac{(\eta-\beta)^{2}t_{k}^{2}}{4(\mu+1)^{2}}\|w^{k}\|^{2},\end{array}

which proves (
106
).

\square

F.13
The Proof of Theorem
7.4

First, since
\Delta=3
, we have
\Theta=12+\Delta r+\hat{\epsilon}=12+3r+\hat{\epsilon}
in Lemma
17
.
Let us choose
\hat{\phi}=\kappa\Theta=\kappa(12+3r+\hat{\epsilon})
in Lemma
17
.
In this case,
M^{2}=((1+\phi)(1+c_{1})+\hat{\phi})L^{2}=(4+12\kappa+3\kappa r+\kappa\hat{\epsilon})L^{2}
in Lemma
12
.

Next, to guarantee
1-\frac{1}{\Delta r}-\hat{\kappa}\Theta>0
, we need to impose
0\leq\hat{\kappa}<\frac{3r-1}{3r(12+3r+\hat{\epsilon})}
, which is exactly the condition (
107
).

Since we require
1-\frac{1}{\Delta r}-\hat{\kappa}\Theta-M^{2}\eta^{2}\geq 0
, we need to choose

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\eta\leq\bar{\eta}:=\frac{\Psi}{L}=\frac{1}{L\sqrt{4+12\kappa+3\kappa r+\kappa\hat{\epsilon}}}\big[1-\frac{1}{3r}-\hat{\kappa}(12+3r+\hat{\epsilon})\big],\end{array}

where
\Psi
is given in (
108
).
Moreover, we have imposed
\eta\geq\frac{\beta(r+\mu-1)}{r-2}
in Lemma
17
, leading to the update rule of
\eta
as in (
109
).
The choice of
\beta:=8\rho+2\epsilon
was also enforced in Lemma
17
for some
\epsilon\geq 0
.

Now, let us denote

\hskip-8.61108pt\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{H}_{k}&:=&\frac{\omega[3(r-2)\psi-(r-1)\beta]t_{k}-\hat{\Gamma}}{2}\|z^{k+1}\|^{2}+\eta\omega M^{2}(\bar{\eta}^{2}-\eta^{2}\big)t_{k}^{2}\|d^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\mu\big(t_{k}-r-\mu-\frac{2\mu\eta}{\omega}\big)\|x^{k+1}-x^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\hat{\epsilon}\omega\eta t_{k+1}^{2}\|z^{k+1}-w^{k+1}\|^{2}+\epsilon\omega t_{k}^{2}\|z^{k+1}-z^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}\hskip-8.61108pt

(174)

Then, under the above parameter selections, (
103
) reduces to

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{k}-\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{k+1}&\geq&\mathcal{H}_{k}-\frac{\omega r^{2}(r-2)^{2}}{\eta t_{k}^{2}}\|x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\geq\mathcal{H}_{k}-\frac{\omega r(r-2)^{2}}{\eta(\mu-1)t_{k}^{2}}\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{k+1},\end{array}

where we have used
\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{k+1}\geq r(\mu-1)\|x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\|^{2}
from (
106
) in the second inequality.

Denote
\tau_{k+1}:=\frac{\omega r(r-2)^{2}}{\eta(\mu-1)t_{k}^{2}}=\frac{\omega r(r-2)^{2}}{\eta(\mu-1)(k+t_{0})^{2}}
.
Then, by the choice of
t_{0}
in (
108
), we have
\tau_{k+1}\in(0,1)
.
Thus the last inequality leads to

\begin{array}[]{lcl}(1-\tau_{k+1})\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{k+1}\leq\big(1+\frac{\tau_{k}}{1-\tau_{k}}\big)(1-\tau_{k})\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{k}-\mathcal{H}_{k}\end{array}

Since
\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\frac{\tau_{k}}{1-\tau_{k}}<\infty
,
(1-\tau_{k})\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{k}\geq 0
, and
\mathcal{H}_{k}\geq 0
, applying
(
9
, Lemma 5.31)
, we can show that
\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\mathcal{H}_{k}<+\infty
and
\lim_{k\to\infty}\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{k}
exists.

Since
t_{k}-r-\mu-\frac{2\mu\eta}{\omega}\geq\frac{t_{k}}{2}
due to the choice of
t_{0}
, the summable result
\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\mathcal{H}_{k}<+\infty
implies the first five summable results in (
110
).
The last summable result follows from
\|w^{k}\|^{2}\leq 2\|z^{k}-w^{k}\|^{2}+2\|z^{k}\|^{2}
.

From the proof of
(
9
, Lemma 5.31)
, we also have

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{k}\leq\frac{1}{(1-\tau_{k})}\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{k-1}\leq\prod_{i=1}^{k}\frac{1}{1-\tau_{i}}\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{0}\leq\prod_{k=1}^{\infty}\frac{1}{1-\tau_{k}}\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{0}.\end{array}

Denoting
\tau:=\prod_{k=1}^{\infty}\frac{1}{1-\tau_{k}}
.
Then, using an elementary proof, we can show that
1\leq\tau\leq\Omega:=\exp\left(\frac{c^{2}}{(1+t_{0})^{2}-c^{2}}\right)\left(\frac{1+t_{0}+c}{1+t_{0}-c}\right)^{\frac{c}{2}}
, where
c^{2}:=\frac{\omega r(r-2)^{2}}{\eta(\mu-1)}
.
Hence, the last inequality leads to
0\leq\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{k}\leq\Omega\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{0}
for all
k\geq 0
.

Finally, since
z^{0}:=w^{0}
and
y^{0}:=x^{0}
, it is obvious to show that
\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{0}\leq\mathcal{R}_{0}^{2}
for
\mathcal{R}_{0}^{2}
defined by (
92
).
By (
106
) and
\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{k}\leq\Omega\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{0}\leq\Omega\mathcal{R}_{0}^{2}
, we have
\frac{(\eta-\beta)^{2}t_{k}^{2}}{4(\mu+1)^{2}}\|w^{k}\|^{2}\leq\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{k}\leq\Omega\mathcal{R}_{0}^{2}
.
This relation implies (
111
).

\square

F.14
The Proof of Theorem
7.5

Denote
\hat{v}^{k}:=y^{k}-x^{k}+\eta_{k-1}z^{k}
.
Similar to the proof of (
161
), we have

\hskip-4.30554pt\begin{array}[]{lcl}t_{k+1}^{2}\|\hat{v}^{k+1}\|^{2}&\leq&t_{k}^{2}\|\hat{v}^{k}\|^{2}-[(r-2)t_{k}+r-1]\|\hat{v}^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{2\eta^{2}(r+1)^{2}t_{k+1}}{(r+2)}\|d^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\frac{2[(r+4)(\eta-\beta)+1]^{2}t_{k+1}}{2(r+2)}\|z^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}\hskip-8.61108pt

This inequality together with (
110
) imply

\begin{array}[]{ll}&\lim_{k\to\infty}t_{k}^{2}\|y^{k}-x^{k}+\eta_{k-1}z^{k}\|^{2}=0,\\
&\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}t_{k}\|y^{k}-x^{k}+\eta_{k-1}z^{k}\|^{2}<+\infty.\end{array}

(175)

Next, we write

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\eta\theta_{k}d^{k}+p^{k}&=&(\eta\theta_{k}-\lambda_{k})\hat{w}^{k+1}+\eta_{k}z^{k+1}-(\eta\theta_{k}\gamma_{k}-\nu_{k})z^{k}\\
&=&-\frac{\eta(r+\mu)}{t_{k+1}}\hat{w}^{k+1}+\frac{[(\eta-\beta)t_{k}-\delta]}{t_{k+1}}z^{k+1}-\big[\frac{(\eta-\beta)\theta_{k}t_{k-1}}{t_{k}}-\frac{s_{k}}{t_{k+1}}\big]z^{k},\end{array}

where
s_{k}:=\frac{[\eta(r-2)+\beta(r+\mu+1)](t_{k}-r-\mu)+(r+\mu)^{2}\beta}{t_{k}}
.

Using this expression and (
GAEG+
), we can show that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\zeta^{k+1}&:=&y^{k+1}-x^{k+1}+\eta_{k}(z^{k+1}-w^{k+1})\\
&=&\theta_{k}[(y^{k}-x^{k})+\eta_{k-1}(z^{k}-w^{k})]-\theta_{k}\eta_{k-1}(z^{k}-w^{k})-\eta\theta_{k}d^{k}\\
&&-{~}p^{k}+\eta_{k}(z^{k+1}-w^{k+1})\\
&=&\theta_{k}\zeta^{k}-\eta_{k}(w^{k+1}-w^{k})+\frac{\eta(r+\mu)}{t_{k+1}}d^{k}+\frac{\hat{s}_{k}}{t_{k+1}}(z^{k}-w^{k})-\frac{\tilde{s}_{k}}{t_{k+1}}z^{k},\end{array}

where
\hat{s}_{k}:=\frac{(r+\mu+1)(\eta-\beta)t_{k}-(r+\mu)(\eta-\beta+\delta)}{t_{k}}\leq\hat{s}:=(r+\mu+1)(\eta-\beta)
and
\tilde{s}_{k}:=\eta(r-1)-\delta:=\tilde{s}>0
.

Denote
h^{k}:=\eta(r+\mu)d^{k}+\hat{s}_{k}(z^{k}-w^{k})-\tilde{s}_{k}z^{k}
.
Using the last expression and Young’s inequality, we can derive

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\|\zeta^{k+1}\|^{2}&=&\theta_{k}^{2}\|\zeta^{k}\|^{2}-2\eta_{k}\theta_{k}\langle w^{k+1}-w^{k},\zeta^{k}\rangle+\frac{2\theta_{k}}{t_{k+1}}\langle\zeta^{k},h^{k}\rangle-\frac{2\eta_{k}}{t_{k+1}}\langle h^{k},w^{k+1}-w^{k}\rangle\\
&&+{~}\eta_{k}^{2}\|w^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{t_{k+1}^{2}}\|h^{k}\|^{2}\\
&\leq&\big(\theta_{k}^{2}+\frac{\theta_{k}}{t_{k+1}}\big)\|\zeta^{k}\|^{2}-2\eta_{k}\theta_{k}\langle w^{k+1}-w^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle\\
&&-{~}2\eta_{k}\theta_{k}\langle w^{k+1}-w^{k},\eta d^{k}+\eta_{k-1}(z^{k}-w^{k})\rangle\\
&&+{}\big(\frac{1}{t_{k+1}^{2}}+\frac{\theta_{k}}{t_{k+1}}+\frac{\eta_{k}}{t_{k+1}}\big)\|h^{k}\|^{2}+\left(\eta_{k}^{2}+\frac{\eta_{k}}{t_{k+1}}\right)\|w^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&\leq&\big(\theta_{k}^{2}+\frac{\theta_{k}}{t_{k+1}}\big)\|\zeta^{k}\|^{2}+\big(2\rho\eta_{k}\theta_{k}+\eta_{k}\theta_{k}+\eta_{k}^{2}+\frac{\eta_{k}}{t_{k+1}}\big)\|w^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}2\eta^{2}\eta_{k}\theta_{k}\|d^{k}\|^{2}+2\eta_{k}\theta_{k}\eta_{k-1}^{2}\|z^{k}-w^{k}\|^{2}+\big(\frac{1}{t_{k+1}^{2}}+\frac{\theta_{k}}{t_{k+1}}+\frac{\eta_{k}}{t_{k+1}}\big)\|h^{k}\|^{2},\end{array}

where we have used
\langle w^{k+1}-w^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle\geq-\rho\|w^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}
from the
\rho
-co-hypomonotonicity of
\Phi
in the last inequality.

Let us denote

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\Sigma_{k}&:=&\big(2\rho\eta_{k}\theta_{k}+\eta_{k}\theta_{k}+\eta_{k}^{2}+\frac{\eta_{k}}{t_{k+1}}\big)\|w^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}+2\eta^{2}\eta_{k}\theta_{k}\|d^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}2\eta_{k}\theta_{k}\eta_{k-1}^{2}\|z^{k}-w^{k}\|^{2}+\big(\frac{1}{t_{k+1}^{2}}+\frac{\theta_{k}}{t_{k+1}}+\frac{\eta_{k}}{t_{k+1}}\big)\|h^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

(176)

Then, the last inequality can be rewritten as

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\|\zeta^{k+1}\|^{2}&\leq&\big(\theta_{k}^{2}+\frac{\theta_{k}}{t_{k+1}}\big)\|\zeta^{k}\|^{2}+\Sigma_{k}.\end{array}

Multiplying this inequality by
t_{k+1}^{2}
, we can show that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}t_{k+1}^{2}\|\zeta^{k+1}\|^{2}&\leq&t_{k}^{2}\|\zeta^{k}\|^{2}+t_{k+1}^{2}\Sigma_{k}\\
&&-{~}[(2r+2\mu-1)t_{k}-(r+\mu)(r+\mu-1)]\|\zeta^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

(177)

Our next step is to prove that
t_{k+1}^{2}\Sigma_{k}
is summable.
Indeed, from (
176
), by Young’s inequality, we have

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\Sigma_{k}&\leq&3\big(2\rho\eta_{k}\theta_{k}+\eta_{k}\theta_{k}+\eta_{k}^{2}+\frac{\eta_{k}}{t_{k+1}}\big)\big[\|z^{k+1}-w^{k+1}\|^{2}+\|z^{k+1}-z^{k}\|^{2}\big]\\
&&+{~}\big[3\big(2\rho\eta_{k}\theta_{k}+\eta_{k}\theta_{k}+\eta_{k}^{2}+\frac{\eta_{k}}{t_{k+1}}\big)+2\eta_{k}\theta_{k}\eta_{k-1}^{2}\big]\|z^{k}-w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\big(\frac{1}{t_{k+1}^{2}}+\frac{\theta_{k}}{t_{k+1}}+\frac{\eta_{k}}{t_{k+1}}\big)\|\eta(r+\mu)d^{k}+\hat{s}_{k}(z^{k}-w^{k})-\tilde{s}_{k}z^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}2\eta^{2}\eta_{k}\theta_{k}\|d^{k}\|^{2}\\
&\leq&3\big(2\rho\eta_{k}\theta_{k}+\eta_{k}\theta_{k}+\eta_{k}^{2}+\frac{\eta_{k}}{t_{k+1}}\big)\big[\|z^{k+1}-w^{k+1}\|^{2}+\|z^{k+1}-z^{k}\|^{2}\big]\\
&&+{~}B_{k}\|z^{k}-w^{k}\|^{2}+3\tilde{s}_{k}^{2}\big(\frac{1}{t_{k+1}^{2}}+\frac{\theta_{k}}{t_{k+1}}+\frac{\eta_{k}}{t_{k+1}}\big)\|z^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}\Big[2\eta^{2}\eta_{k}\theta_{k}+3\eta^{2}(r+\mu)^{2}\big(\frac{1}{t_{k+1}^{2}}+\frac{\theta_{k}}{t_{k+1}}+\frac{\eta_{k}}{t_{k+1}}\big)\Big]\|d^{k}\|^{2},\end{array}

where
B_{k}:=3\big(2\rho\eta_{k}\theta_{k}+\eta_{k}\theta_{k}+\eta_{k}^{2}+\frac{\eta_{k}}{t_{k+1}}\big)+2\eta_{k}\theta_{k}\eta_{k-1}^{2}+3\hat{s}_{k}^{2}\big(\frac{1}{t_{k+1}^{2}}+\frac{\theta_{k}}{t_{k+1}}+\frac{\eta_{k}}{t_{k+1}}\big)
.

Using the facts that
\eta_{k}\leq\frac{(\eta-\beta)t_{k}}{t_{k+1}}\leq\eta-\beta
,
\theta_{k}\leq\frac{t_{k}}{t_{k+1}}
,
\hat{s}_{k}\leq\hat{s}:=(r+\mu+1)(\eta-\beta)
, and
\tilde{s}_{k}=\tilde{s}:=\eta(r-1)-\delta
, we can prove that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}t_{k+1}^{2}\Sigma_{k}&\leq&3(\eta-\beta)\big[(2\rho+1+\eta-\beta)t_{k}^{2}+t_{k}\big]\big[\|z^{k+1}-w^{k+1}\|^{2}+\|z^{k+1}-z^{k}\|^{2}\big]\\
&&+{~}\Big[2\eta^{2}(\eta-\beta)t_{k}^{2}+3\eta^{2}(r+\mu)^{2}(\eta-\beta+1)t_{k}+3\eta^{2}(r+\mu)^{2}\Big]\|d^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}3\tilde{s}^{2}\big[(\eta-\beta+1)t_{k}+1\big]\|z^{k}\|^{2}+\tilde{B}_{k}\|z^{k}-w^{k}\|^{2},\end{array}

where
\tilde{B}_{k}:=(\eta-\beta)\big[(6\rho+3+3\eta-3\beta+2(\eta-\beta)^{2})t_{k}^{2}+3t_{k}\big]+3[\hat{s}^{2}(\eta-\beta+1)+\eta-\beta]t_{k}+3\hat{s}^{2}=\mathcal{O}\left(t_{k}^{2}\right)
.

Now, applying (
110
) to this inequality, one can establish that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}t_{k+1}^{2}\Sigma_{k}<+\infty.\end{array}

(178)

Then, applying (
178
), we can prove from (
177
) that

\begin{array}[]{ll}&\lim_{k\to\infty}t_{k}^{2}\|y^{k}-x^{k}+\eta_{k-1}(z^{k}-w^{k})\|^{2}=0,\\
&\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}t_{k}\|y^{k}-x^{k}+\eta_{k-1}(z^{k}-w^{k})\|^{2}<+\infty.\end{array}

(179)

In addition, by Young’s inequality, we get

\begin{array}[]{ll}\eta_{k-1}^{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}&\leq 2\|y^{k}-x^{k}+\eta_{k-1}(z^{k}-w^{k})\|^{2}+2\|y^{k}-x^{k}+\eta_{k-1}z^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Using (
176
) and (
179
) into this inequality, we obtain
\lim_{k\to\infty}t_{k}^{2}\eta_{k-1}^{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}=0
.
Since
\eta_{k-1}=\frac{(\eta-\beta)t_{k-1}-\delta}{t_{k}}=\eta-\beta-\frac{(\eta-\beta-\delta)}{t_{k}}\geq\delta
as
t_{k}\geq 1
, the last limit leads to
\lim_{k\to\infty}t_{k}^{2}\|w^{k}\|^{2}=0
, which proves the first line of (
112
).

By Young’s inequality again, we also have

\begin{array}[]{lcl}t_{k}^{2}\|y^{k}-x^{k}\|^{2}&\leq&2t_{k}^{2}\|y^{k}-x^{k}+\eta_{k-1}(z^{k}-w^{k})\|^{2}+2\eta_{k-1}^{2}t_{k}^{2}\|z^{k}-w^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

This inequality together with (
179
) and the summability of
t_{k}^{2}\|z^{k}-w^{k}\|^{2}
imply the second line of (
112
).

Finally, since
t_{k}^{2}\|x^{k+1}-x^{k}\|^{2}\leq 2\eta^{2}t_{k}^{2}\|d^{k}\|^{2}+2t_{k}^{2}\|y^{k}-x^{k}\|^{2}
, applying this relation, the second line of (
112
), and the summability of
t_{k}^{2}\|d^{k}\|^{2}
, we get the third line of (
112
).

\square

F.15
Technical Lemma
21
and Lemma
22

First, let us define the following quantities:

\hskip-4.30554pt\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{A}_{k}&:=&\|r(x^{k}-x^{\star})+t_{k}(y^{k}-x^{k})+t_{k}\eta_{k-1}(z^{k}-w^{k})\|^{2}+r\mu\|x^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2},\\
\hat{\mathcal{Q}}_{k}&:=&\mathcal{A}_{k}+2rt_{k}\eta_{k-1}\big[\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle+\rho\|w^{k}\|^{2}].\end{array}\hskip-8.61108pt

(180)

Clearly, by the
\rho
-co-hypomonotonicity of
\Phi
, we have
\hat{\mathcal{Q}}_{k}\geq 0
.

Lemma 21

Under the same settings as in Theorem
7.5
,
\hat{\mathcal{Q}}_{k}
defined by (
180
) satisfies the following inequality:

\hskip-8.61108pt\begin{array}[]{lcl}\hat{\mathcal{Q}}_{k}-\hat{\mathcal{Q}}_{k+1}&\geq&\mu(2t_{k}-r-\mu)\|x^{k+1}-x^{k}\|^{2}-2[(\eta-\beta)t_{k}-\delta]^{2}\|z^{k+1}-w^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}2t_{k+1}^{2}\|p^{k}\|^{2}-2\rho rt_{k+1}\eta_{k}\|w^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}2[(\eta-\beta)t_{k}-\delta](t_{k}-r-\mu)\langle w^{k+1}-w^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle\\
&&+{~}2r[\eta(r-2)+\beta-\delta]\langle z^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\rangle+2\mu\eta t_{k}\langle\hat{w}^{k+1},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle\\
&&+{~}2s^{3}_{k}\langle z^{k}-w^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle-2\hat{s}^{3}_{k}\langle z^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle,\end{array}\hskip-38.74988pt

(181)

where

\left\{\begin{array}[]{lcl}s^{3}_{k}&:=&(\eta-\beta)(r+\mu-1)t_{k}-(r+\mu)\delta,\\
\hat{s}^{3}_{k}&:=&[(\mu+1)\eta+\delta-r\beta]t_{k}-r(2\eta-\beta+2\delta-r\eta)-\delta\mu.\end{array}\right.

Proof

Denote
g^{k}:=\eta d^{k}+\eta_{k-1}(z^{k}-w^{k})
.
Using this
g^{k}
and
x^{k+1}=y^{k}-\eta d^{k}
from (
GAEG+
) and (
76
), similar to the proof of Lemma
10
, we have

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\breve{\mathcal{T}}_{[1]}&:=&\|r(x^{k}-x^{\star})+t_{k}(y^{k}-x^{k})+t_{k}\eta_{k-1}(z^{k}-w^{k})\|^{2}\\
&=&r^{2}\|x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\|^{2}+(t_{k}-r)^{2}\|x^{k+1}-x^{k}\|^{2}+t_{k}^{2}\|g^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}2r(t_{k}-r)\langle x^{k+1}-x^{\star},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle+2rt_{k}\langle g^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&&+{~}2t_{k}(t_{k}-r)\langle g^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle.\end{array}

Alternatively, denote
h^{k}:=p^{k}-\eta_{k}(z^{k+1}-w^{k+1})
.
Then, similar to the proof of Lemma
10
, we can show from (
GAEG+
) that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\breve{\mathcal{T}}_{[2]}&:=&\|r(x^{k+1}-x^{\star})+t_{k+1}(y^{k+1}-x^{k+1})+t_{k+1}\eta_{k}(z^{k+1}-w^{k+1})\|^{2}\\
&=&r^{2}\|x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\|^{2}+t_{k+1}^{2}\theta_{k}^{2}\|x^{k+1}-x^{k}\|^{2}+t_{k+1}^{2}\|h^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}2rt_{k+1}\theta_{k}\langle x^{k+1}-x^{\star},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle-2rt_{k+1}\langle h^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&&-{~}2t_{k+1}^{2}\theta_{k}\langle h^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle.\end{array}

Combining both expressions
\breve{\mathcal{T}}_{[1]}
and
\breve{\mathcal{T}}_{[2]}
, and the identity
\mu r\|x^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}-\mu r\|x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\|^{2}=\mu r\|x^{k+1}-x^{k}\|^{2}-2r\mu\langle x^{k+1}-x^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\rangle
, we can derive from (
180
) that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{A}_{k}-\mathcal{A}_{k+1}&=&\mu(2t_{k}-r-\mu)\|x^{k+1}-x^{k}\|^{2}+t_{k}^{2}\|g^{k}\|^{2}-t_{k+1}^{2}\|h^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}2\langle t_{k}(t_{k}-r)g^{k}+t_{k+1}^{2}\theta_{k}h^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle\\
&&+{~}2r\langle t_{k}g^{k}+t_{k+1}h^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\rangle.\end{array}

(182)

Next, we note that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}t_{k}g^{k}+t_{k+1}h^{k}&=&\eta t_{k}\hat{w}^{k+1}-\eta t_{k}\gamma_{k}z^{k}+t_{k}\eta_{k-1}(z^{k}-w^{k})+t_{k+1}\eta_{k}z^{k+1}\\
&&-{~}t_{k+1}\lambda_{k}\hat{w}^{k+1}+t_{k+1}\nu_{k}z^{k}-t_{k+1}\eta_{k}(z^{k+1}-w^{k+1})\\
&=&(\eta t_{k}-t_{k+1}\lambda_{k})\hat{w}^{k+1}+t_{k+1}\eta_{k}w^{k+1}-t_{k}\eta_{k-1}w^{k}\\
&&-{~}(\eta t_{k}\gamma_{k}-t_{k+1}\nu_{k}-t_{k}\eta_{k-1})z^{k}\\
&=&t_{k+1}\eta_{k}w^{k+1}-t_{k}\eta_{k-1}w^{k}+[\eta(r-2)+\beta-\delta]z^{k}.\end{array}

Therefore, one can prove that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\breve{\mathcal{T}}_{[3]}&:=&\langle t_{k}g^{k}+t_{k+1}h^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&=&t_{k+1}\eta_{k}\langle w^{k+1},x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\rangle-t_{k}\eta_{k-1}\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&&-{~}t_{k}\eta_{k-1}\langle w^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle-(\eta r+\beta-\delta)\langle z^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&=&t_{k+1}\eta_{k}\langle w^{k+1},x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\rangle-t_{k}\eta_{k-1}\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&&+{~}t_{k}\eta_{k-1}\langle z^{k}-w^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle-t_{k}\eta_{k-1}\langle z^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle\\
&&+{~}[\eta(r-2)+\beta-\delta]\langle z^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\rangle.\end{array}

Similarly, we also get

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\breve{\mathcal{T}}_{[4]}&:=&t_{k}(t_{k}-r)g^{k}+t_{k+1}^{2}\theta_{k}h^{k}\\
&=&\eta t_{k}(t_{k}-r)\hat{w}^{k+1}-\eta t_{k}(t_{k}-r)\gamma_{k}z^{k}+t_{k}(t_{k}-r)\eta_{k-1}(z^{k}-w^{k})\\
&&+{~}t_{k+1}^{2}\theta_{k}\eta_{k}z^{k+1}-t_{k+1}^{2}\theta_{k}\lambda_{k}\hat{w}^{k+1}+t_{k+1}^{2}\theta_{k}\nu_{k}z^{k}-t_{k+1}^{2}\theta_{k}\eta_{k}(z^{k+1}-w^{k+1})\\
&=&\mu\eta t_{k}\hat{w}^{k+1}+[(\eta-\beta)t_{k}-\delta](t_{k}-r-\mu)(w^{k+1}-w^{k})\\
&&+{~}[(\mu-1)(\eta-\beta)t_{k}-\mu\delta+r(\eta-\beta)](z^{k}-w^{k})\\
&&+{~}\big\{[\eta(r-\mu-1)-\delta]t_{k}-\eta r(r-1)+\delta(r+\mu)\big\}z^{k}.\end{array}

This expression leads to

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\breve{\mathcal{T}}_{[5]}&:=&\langle t_{k}(t_{k}-r)g^{k}+t_{k+1}^{2}\theta_{k}h^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle\\
&=&\mu\eta t_{k}\langle\hat{w}^{k+1},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle+[(\eta-\beta)t_{k}-\delta](t_{k}-r-\mu)\langle w^{k+1}-w^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle\\
&&+{~}[(\mu-1)(\eta-\beta)t_{k}-\mu\delta+r(\eta-\beta)]\langle z^{k}-w^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle\\
&&+{~}\big\{[\eta(r-\mu-1)-\delta]t_{k}-\eta r(r-1)+\delta(r+\mu)\big\}\langle z^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle.\end{array}

Substituting
\breve{\mathcal{T}}_{[3]}
and
\breve{\mathcal{T}}_{[5]}
into (
182
), we can show that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{A}_{k}-\mathcal{A}_{k+1}&=&\mu(2t_{k}-r-\mu)\|x^{k+1}-x^{k}\|^{2}+t_{k}^{2}\|g^{k}\|^{2}-t_{k+1}^{2}\|h^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}2rt_{k+1}\eta_{k}\langle w^{k+1},x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\rangle-2rt_{k}\eta_{k-1}\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&&+{~}2r[\eta(r-2)+\beta-\delta]\langle z^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\rangle+2\mu\eta t_{k}\langle\hat{w}^{k+1},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle\\
&&+{~}2[(\eta-\beta)t_{k}-\delta](t_{k}-r-\mu)\langle w^{k+1}-w^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle\\
&&+{~}2s^{3}_{k}\langle z^{k}-w^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle-2\hat{s}^{3}_{k}\langle z^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle,\end{array}

where

\begin{array}[]{lcl}s^{3}_{k}&:=&(\eta-\beta)(r+\mu-1)t_{k}-(r+\mu)\delta\\
\hat{s}^{3}_{k}&:=&[(\mu+1)\eta+\delta-r\beta]t_{k}-r(2\eta-\beta+2\delta-r\eta)-\delta\mu.\end{array}

Finally, using the definition of
\hat{\mathcal{Q}}_{k}
from (
180
) and
t_{k+1}^{2}\|h^{k}\|^{2}\leq 2t_{k+1}^{2}\|p^{k}\|^{2}+2[(\eta-\beta)t_{k}-\delta]^{2}\|z^{k+1}-w^{k+1}\|^{2}
, and neglecting the nonnegative terms
t_{k}^{2}\|g^{k}\|^{2}
and
2\rho rt_{k}\eta_{k-1}\|w^{k}\|^{2}
, the last expression leads to (
181
).

\square

Lemma 22

Under the same settings as in Theorem
7.5
, we have

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}t_{k}|\langle z^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle|&<&+\infty,\\
\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}t_{k}|\langle\hat{w}^{k+1},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle|&<&+\infty,\\
\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}t_{k}|\langle z^{k}-w^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle|&<&+\infty,\\
\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}t_{k}^{2}\|w^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}&<&+\infty,\\
\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}t_{k}^{2}\|p^{k}\|^{2}&<&+\infty.\end{array}

(183)

In addition, for
\Omega
given in Theorem
7.4
, and
\mathcal{R}_{0}^{2}
in (
92
), we also have

\begin{array}[]{lcl}2\langle z^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\rangle&\geq&-2\rho\|w^{k}\|^{2}-\|z^{k}\|^{2}-\|x^{k+1}-x^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&-{~}t_{k}^{2}\|z^{k}-w^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{\Omega\mathcal{R}_{0}^{2}}{r(\mu-1)t_{k}^{2}}.\end{array}

(184)

Proof

Since
2t_{k}|\langle z^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle|\leq t_{k}\|z^{k}\|^{2}+t_{k}\|x^{k+1}-x^{k}\|^{2}
, we obtain the first line of (
183
) from this relation and the summable results in (
110
).

Next, we have
\|\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}\leq 2\|d^{k}\|^{2}+2\gamma_{k}^{2}\|z^{k}\|^{2}\leq 2\|d^{k}\|^{2}+2\|z^{k}\|^{2}
.
Therefore, we can show that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}2t_{k}|\langle\hat{w}^{k+1},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle|&\leq&t_{k}\|\hat{w}^{k+1}\|^{2}+t_{k}\|x^{k+1}-x^{k}\|^{2}\\
&\leq&2t_{k}\|d^{k}\|^{2}+2t_{k}\|z^{k}\|^{2}+t_{k}\|x^{k+1}-x^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

This inequality together with the summable results in (
110
) imply the second line of (
183
).
The third line of (
183
) is proven similarly, and hence, we omit.

Since
\|w^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}\leq 3\|z^{k+1}-w^{k+1}\|^{2}+3\|z^{k+1}-z^{k}\|^{2}+3\|z^{k}-w^{k}\|^{2}
due to Young’s inequality, we obtain the fourth line of (
183
) by combining this inequality and (
110
).

We also have

\begin{array}[]{lcl}t_{k+1}^{2}\|p^{k}\|^{2}&\leq&3[(\eta-\beta)t_{k}-\delta]^{2}\|z^{k+1}-z^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}3\eta^{2}t_{k}^{2}\|d^{k}\|^{2}+3[\eta(r-1)-\delta]^{2}\|z^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Since all the terms on the right-hand side of this inequality are summable due to (
110
), we obtain the last line of (
183
).

Finally, by Young’s inequality, we have

\begin{array}[]{lcl}2\langle z^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{\star}\rangle&=&2\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle+2\langle z^{k}-w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle+2\langle z^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle\\
&\geq&-2\rho\|w^{k}\|^{2}-2\|z^{k}-w^{k}\|\|x^{k}-x^{\star}\|-\|z^{k}\|^{2}-\|x^{k+1}-x^{k}\|^{2}\\
&\geq&-2\rho\|w^{k}\|^{2}-t_{k}^{2}\|z^{k}-w^{k}\|^{2}-\frac{\|x^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}}{t_{k}^{2}}\\
&&-{~}\|z^{k}\|^{2}-\|x^{k+1}-x^{k}\|^{2}.\end{array}

Since
r(\mu-1)\|x^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\leq\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{k}\leq\Omega\hat{\mathcal{L}}_{0}\leq\Omega\mathcal{R}_{0}^{2}
as in the proof of Theorem
7.4
, the last inequality implies (
184
).

\square

F.16
The Proof of Theorem
7.6

From the proof of Theorem
7.4
, we have
r(\mu-1)\|x^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}\leq\mathcal{L}_{k}\leq\Omega\mathcal{R}_{0}^{2}
, we conclude that
\{x^{k}\}
is bounded, and hence, it has a cluster point.
Let
x^{*}
be a cluster point of
x^{k}
and
x^{k_{i}}
be a subsequence converging to
x^{*}
.
Similar to the proof of Theorem
7.3
, we have
0\in Fx^{*}+Tx^{*}
(i.e.
x^{*}\in\mathrm{zer}(\Phi)
).

Our next step is to show that
\lim_{k\to\infty}\|x^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}
exists.
Combining (
181
), (
183
), and (
184
), we can derive that

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\hat{\mathcal{Q}}_{k+1}-\hat{\mathcal{Q}}_{k}&\leq&t_{k+1}^{2}\|p^{k}\|^{2}+2[(\eta-\beta)t_{k}-\delta]^{2}\|z^{k+1}-w^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}2\rho[(\eta-\beta)t_{k}-\delta](t_{k}-r-\mu)\|w^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}r[\eta(r-2)+\beta-\delta]\Big[2\rho\|w^{k}\|^{2}+\|z^{k}\|^{2}+\|x^{k+1}-x^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+{~}t_{k}^{2}\|z^{k}-w^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{\Omega\mathcal{R}_{0}^{2}}{r(\mu-1)t_{k}^{2}}\Big]\\
&&+{~}2\mu\eta t_{k}|\langle\hat{w}^{k+1},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle|+2\rho rt_{k+1}\eta_{k}\|w^{k+1}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}2s^{3}_{k}|\langle z^{k}-w^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle|+2\hat{s}^{3}_{k}|\langle z^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle|,\end{array}

where we have used
\langle w^{k+1}-w^{k},x^{k+1}-x^{k}\rangle\geq-\rho\|w^{k+1}-w^{k}\|^{2}
.

It is clear that since
s_{k}^{3}=\mathcal{O}\left(t_{k}\right)
and
\hat{s}^{3}_{k}=\mathcal{O}\left(t_{k}\right)
, all the terms on the right-hand sides of the last inequality are summable due to (
110
) and (
183
).
Moreover, we have
\hat{\mathcal{Q}}_{k}\geq 0
.
Applying
(
9
, Lemma 5.31)
, we can conclude that the limit
\lim_{k\to\infty}\hat{\mathcal{Q}}_{k}
exists.

Similar to (
165
), we have
\lim_{k\to\infty}|t_{k}\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle|=0
.
Since
\hat{\mathcal{Q}}_{k}=\mathcal{A}_{k}+2rt_{k}\eta_{k-1}\big[\langle w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle+\rho\|w^{k}\|^{2}\big]
, this limit also implies that
\lim_{k\to\infty}\mathcal{A}_{k}
exists.

From the definition of
\mathcal{A}_{k}
in (
180
), we can expand it as

\begin{array}[]{lcl}\mathcal{A}_{k}&=&(r^{2}+\mu r)\|x^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}+t_{k}^{2}\|y^{k}-x^{k}\|^{2}+t_{k}^{2}\eta_{k-1}^{2}\|z^{k}-w^{k}\|^{2}\\
&&+{~}2rt_{k}\langle x^{k}-x^{\star},y^{k}-x^{k}\rangle+2rt_{k}\eta_{k-1}\langle z^{k}-w^{k},x^{k}-x^{\star}\rangle\\
&&+{~}2t_{k}^{2}\eta_{k-1}\langle y^{k}-x^{k},z^{k}-w^{k}\rangle.\end{array}

It is not hard to show that the limits of the last five terms are all zeros.
Combining these facts and the existence of
\lim_{k\to\infty}\mathcal{A}_{k}
, we conclude that
\lim_{k\to\infty}\|x^{k}-x^{\star}\|^{2}
exists.
Thus we have
\lim_{k\to\infty}x^{k}=x^{\star}\in\mathrm{zer}(\Phi)
due to the well-known Opial’s lemma.
Finally, since
\lim_{k\to\infty}\|y^{k}-x^{k}\|^{2}=0
, we also have
\lim_{k\to\infty}y^{k}=x^{\star}\in\mathrm{zer}(\Phi)
.

\square
